import os
import glob
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QTreeWidget, QTreeWidgetItem, 
                             QLabel, QHeaderView, QPushButton, QMessageBox, QHBoxLayout)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from src.ui.compare_results import ComparisonPlotWindow

class CompareWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("Compare simulation logs")
        self.resize(900, 600)
        
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # 1. Header
        lbl_title = QLabel("Select simulations to compare")
        lbl_title.setStyleSheet("font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 5px;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_title)
        
        lbl_hint = QLabel("Check the boxes on the left. All selected logs must be from the SAME track.")
        lbl_hint.setStyleSheet("color: #666; margin-bottom: 10px;")
        layout.addWidget(lbl_hint)
        
        # 2. Table (TreeWidget)
        self.tree = QTreeWidget()
        # První sloupec bude mít checkbox
        self.tree.setHeaderLabels(["Track Name", "Lap Time (s)", "Car Config", "File / Date"])
        
        # Nastavení šířky sloupců
        header = self.tree.header()
        header.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents) # Track
        header.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents) # Time
        header.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents) # Car
        
        self.tree.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
        # Root decoration schováme (nemáme zanořování, je to plochý seznam)
        self.tree.setRootIsDecorated(False) 
        
        layout.addWidget(self.tree)
        
        # 3. Buttons Area
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        self.btn_refresh = QPushButton("Refresh List")
        self.btn_refresh.clicked.connect(self.load_logs)
        
        self.btn_compare = QPushButton("Compare Selected")
        self.btn_compare.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_compare.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #007bff; 
                color: white; 
                font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 10px;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #0056b3;
            }
        """)
        self.btn_compare.clicked.connect(self.on_compare_clicked)
        
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_refresh)
        btn_layout.addStretch() # Mezera
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_compare)
        
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

        # Načtení dat při startu
        self.load_logs()

    def load_logs(self):
        """Proskenuje složku results a naplní tabulku s checkboxy."""
        self.tree.clear()
        results_dir = "results"
        
        if not os.path.exists(results_dir):
            os.makedirs(results_dir)
            return

        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(results_dir, "*.npz"))
        logs_data = []

        # A) Načtení metadat
        for filepath in files:
            try:
                data = np.load(filepath, allow_pickle=True)
                
                track_name = str(data['track_name']) if 'track_name' in data else "Unknown"
                lap_time = float(data['lap_time']) if 'lap_time' in data else 0.0
                car_name = str(data['car_name']) if 'car_name' in data else "-"
                
                # Zformátujeme datum ze jména souboru (pokud to jde), jinak jen filename
                filename = os.path.basename(filepath)
                
                logs_data.append({
                    'track': track_name,
                    'time': lap_time,
                    'car': car_name,
                    'file': filename,
                    'path': filepath
                })
                data.close()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error reading {filepath}: {e}")
                continue

        # B) Třídění (Track -> Time)
        logs_data.sort(key=lambda x: (x['track'], x['time']))

        # C) Vykreslení
        for item in logs_data:
            row = QTreeWidgetItem([
                item['track'],
                f"{item['time']:.3f}",
                item['car'],
                item['file']
            ])
            
            # --- PŘIDÁNÍ CHECKBOXU ---
            # Nastavíme checkbox do sloupce 0 (Track Name)
            row.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Unchecked)
            
            # Uložíme si cestu k souboru do skrytých dat
            row.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, item['path'])
            
            self.tree.addTopLevelItem(row)

    def on_compare_clicked(self):
        """Zkontroluje výběr a spustí porovnání."""
        selected_data = []
        track_names_set = set()
        
        # 1. Iterace přes všechny řádky
        root = self.tree.invisibleRootItem()
        count = root.childCount()
        
        for i in range(count):
            item = root.child(i)
            
            # Je checkbox zaškrtnutý?
            if item.checkState(0) == Qt.CheckState.Checked:
                path = item.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)
                track_name = item.text(0)
                lap_time = item.text(1)
                filename = item.text(3)
                
                selected_data.append({
                    'path': path,
                    'track': track_name,
                    'legend': f"{filename} ({lap_time}s)"
                })
                
                track_names_set.add(track_name)

        # 2. VALIDACE: Počet logů
        if len(selected_data) < 2:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Not enough data", 
                                "Please select at least 2 logs to compare.")
            return

        # 3. VALIDACE: Shoda tratí
        if len(track_names_set) > 1:
            tracks_list = ", ".join(track_names_set)
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Track Mismatch", 
                                 f"You cannot compare logs from different tracks!\n\n"
                                 f"Selected tracks: {tracks_list}\n"
                                 f"Please select logs from the same track only.")
            return

        # 4. Success -> Spustit vizualizaci
        # (Zatím jen print, příště uděláme okno)
        print(f"Comparing {len(selected_data)} logs form track '{list(track_names_set)[0]}'...")
        print(f"Comparing {len(selected_data)} logs...")
        
        # Uložíme do self, aby okno nezmizelo
        self.comparison_window = ComparisonPlotWindow(selected_data)
        self.comparison_window.show()
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\ui\results_tab.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\results_window.py ---
import datetime
import numpy as np
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
                             QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QGridLayout, QFileDialog, QDoubleSpinBox,QApplication,QPushButton)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
import pyqtgraph as pg
from src.utils.saver import save_results_to_npz
import os

class ResultsWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, res, car, track,ds):
        super().__init__()
        self.res = res
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.ds = ds

        self.resize(1100, 800)
        
        self.center_window()
        self.setWindowTitle(f"Simulation Results - {res.lap_time:.3f} s")
     
        
        #main layout
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # 1. Summary panel 
        self.init_summary_panel()
        
        # 2. Tabs with graphs
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        self.init_dynamics_tab()
        self.init_powertrain_tab()
        self.init_forces_tab()
        self.init_sensitivity_tab()
        self.init_map_tab()
        self.init_drs_map_tab()
        self.init_trajectory_tab()
        self.init_soc_tab()

    def center_window(self):
        """centers where is mice"""
        # get frame geometry
        qr = self.frameGeometry()
        # get the mice position
        cp = QApplication.primaryScreen().availableGeometry().center()

        qr.moveCenter(cp)
        
        self.move(qr.topLeft())
    
        self.showNormal()

    def init_summary_panel(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Lap Summary")
        main_h_layout = QHBoxLayout() 
        
        # left part
        stats_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(stats_widget)
        grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        def add_stat(row, col, label, value, unit):
            lbl_name = QLabel(f"<b>{label}:</b>")
            lbl_val = QLabel(f"{value}")
            lbl_val.setStyleSheet("color: #007bff; font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;")
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, col)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_val, row, col+1)
            grid.addWidget(QLabel(unit), row, col+2)

        max_v = np.max(self.res.v) * 3.6
        avg_v = np.mean(self.res.v) * 3.6
        total_energy = self.res.E[-1]
        
        add_stat(0, 0, "Lap Time", f"{self.res.lap_time:.3f}", "s")
        add_stat(0, 3, "Energy Used", f"{total_energy:.3f}", "kWh")
        add_stat(1, 0, "Max Speed", f"{max_v:.1f}", "km/h")
        add_stat(1, 3, "Avg Speed", f"{avg_v:.1f}", "km/h")
        
        main_h_layout.addWidget(stats_widget)
        
        # Save Button and spacer
        main_h_layout.addStretch() # puts the button to right
        
        self.btn_save = QPushButton("Save Results (.npz)")
        self.btn_save.setFixedWidth(150)
        self.btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #17a2b8; 
                color: white; 
                font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 8px; 
                border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #138496;
            }
        """)
        self.btn_save.clicked.connect(self.on_save_clicked)
        
        main_h_layout.addWidget(self.btn_save)
        
        group.setLayout(main_h_layout)
        self.layout.addWidget(group)

    def calculate_validation_metrics(self,sim_res, ref_data):
        """
        Porovná výsledky simulace s referenčními daty.
        
        Args:
            sim_res: SolverResults (obsahuje .s, .t, .v ze solveru)
            ref_data: Dict {'s': ..., 'v': ..., 'p': ...} (Reference z Tracku)
                    Předpoklad: 'v' je v m/s, 'p' je v kW
        """
        if ref_data is None or ref_data['v'] is None:
            return None

        # 1. Získání os (Solver vs Reference)
        s_sim = sim_res.s          # Mřížka solveru (např. po 0.5m)
        s_ref = ref_data['s']      # Mřížka trati (např. z logu)
        v_ref_raw = ref_data['v']  # Rychlost na mřížce trati [m/s]
        
        # 2. DOPOČET REFERENČNÍHO ČASU (Integrace: dt = ds / v)
        # Musíme to udělat na mřížce reference, než budeme interpolovat
        ds_ref = np.diff(s_ref, prepend=0)
        
        # Ošetření dělení nulou (pokud auto v logu stálo nebo jelo super pomalu)
        # Cokoliv pod 1.0 m/s nahradíme 1.0 m/s pro výpočet času, jinak čas vyletí do nekonečna
        v_safe = np.maximum(v_ref_raw, 1.0) 
        
        dt_ref = ds_ref / v_safe
        t_ref_calculated = np.cumsum(dt_ref) # Kumulativní součet -> Časová osa reference

        # 3. INTERPOLACE NA MŘÍŽKU SOLVERU
        # Abychom mohli odečítat (Sim - Real), musí mít vektory stejnou délku
        v_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, v_ref_raw)
        t_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, t_ref_calculated)
        
        # Power (volitelně) - Log je v kW, Solver je ve Wattech -> Převedeme log na W nebo Sim na kW
        # Zde převedeme interp log na Watty pro konzistenci výpočtů, v grafu to pak dáme na kW
        p_ref_interp = None
        if ref_data.get('p') is not None:
            p_ref_interp = np.interp(s_sim, s_ref, ref_data['p']) * 1000.0 # kW -> W

        # 4. VÝPOČET DELT
        # Delta V: Kladné = Sim je rychlejší
        delta_v = sim_res.v - v_ref_interp 
        
        # Delta T: Kladné = Sim je pomalejší (ztrácí čas)
        # Resetujeme start na 0, abychom porovnávali čistý průjezd kola
        t_sim_zeroed = sim_res.t - sim_res.t[0]
        t_ref_zeroed = t_ref_interp - t_ref_interp[0]
        
        delta_t = t_sim_zeroed - t_ref_zeroed

        # 5. METRIKY
        rmse_v = np.sqrt(np.mean(delta_v**2))
        total_time_diff = t_sim_zeroed[-1] - t_ref_zeroed[-1]
        
        return {
            's': s_sim,
            'v_ref': v_ref_interp,   # [m/s] aligned
            't_ref': t_ref_zeroed,   # [s] aligned
            'p_ref': p_ref_interp,   # [W] aligned
            'delta_v': delta_v,      # [m/s]
            'delta_t': delta_t,      # [s]
            'rmse_v': rmse_v,
            'diff_total': total_time_diff
        }

    def on_save_clicked(self):
        """opens dialog and saves the results."""
       
        now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M")
        default_name = f"{self.track.name}_{now_str}.npz"
        
        # results folder
        if not os.path.exists("results"):
            os.makedirs("results")
            
        default_path = os.path.join("results", default_name)

        # QFileDialog
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self,
            "Save Simulation Results",
            default_path,
            "NumPy Zip (*.npz)"
        )

        if filepath:
            try:
                save_results_to_npz(
                    filepath, 
                    self.res, 
                    self.track, 
                    self.ds
                )
               
                self.btn_save.setText("Saved!")
                self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #28a745; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px;")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error saving file: {e}")
                self.btn_save.setText("Error!")
                self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("background-color: #dc3545; color: white;")

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # --- 1. SPEED GRAPH ---
        plot_v = pg.PlotWidget(title="Velocity Comparison")
        plot_v.setLabel('left', 'Speed', units='km/h')
        plot_v.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        #plot_v.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        plot_v.addLegend()
      
        # Simulation (Blue)
        plot_v.plot(self.res.s, self.res.v * 3.6, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2), name="Simulation")
        
        # Validation Logic
        val_data = None
        if self.track.ref_data is not None:
           
            val_data = self.calculate_validation_metrics(self.res, self.track.ref_data)
            
            if val_data:
                # Reference (Black Dashed)
                v_ref_kmh = val_data['v_ref'] * 3.6
                plot_v.plot(val_data['s'], v_ref_kmh, 
                           pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine), 
                           name=f"Real Log (RMSE: {val_data['rmse_v']*3.6:.1f} km/h)")

        layout.addWidget(plot_v)

        # --- 2. DELTA TIME GRAPH ---
        plot_dt = pg.PlotWidget(title="Delta Time (Accumulated Gap)")
        plot_dt.setLabel('left', 'Delta Time', units='s')
        plot_dt.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        #plot_dt.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        plot_dt.setXLink(plot_v)
        
        # Zero Line (Vizuální linka)
        plot_dt.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=0, angle=0, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=1)))

        if val_data:
            # 1. Vykreslíme křivku delty a uložíme si ji do proměnné
            dt_curve = plot_dt.plot(val_data['s'], val_data['delta_t'], pen=pg.mkPen('r', width=2))
            
            # 2. Vytvoříme pomocnou "neviditelnou" nulovou křivku pro výplň
            # (FillBetweenItem funguje nejlépe mezi dvěma PlotCurveItems)
            dummy_zero = pg.PlotCurveItem(val_data['s'], np.zeros_like(val_data['s']), pen=None)
            plot_dt.addItem(dummy_zero)
            
            # 3. Vytvoříme výplň mezi Delta křivkou a nulou
            # Používáme přímé reference 'dt_curve' a 'dummy_zero' - ŽÁDNÉ INDEXY!
            fill = pg.FillBetweenItem(dt_curve, dummy_zero, brush=(255, 0, 0, 50))
            plot_dt.addItem(fill)
            
            # Title update
            diff = val_data['diff_total']
            sign = "+" if diff > 0 else ""
            plot_dt.setTitle(f"Delta Time (Final Gap: {sign}{diff:.3f} s)")
        else:
            plot_dt.setTitle("No reference data available")

        layout.addWidget(plot_dt)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics & Validation")

    def init_trajectory_tab(self):
        """
        Porovná ideální centerline (Track) a optimalizovanou stopu (Car).
        Rekonstruuje XY pozici auta pomocí referenční čáry a odchylky 'n'.
        Přidává graf steering angle.
        """
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # --- 1. Výpočet souřadnic auta ---
        if not hasattr(self.res, 'n') or len(self.res.n) == 0:
            layout.addWidget(QLabel("Error: 'n' (lateral deviation) missing in SolverResults."))
            widget.setLayout(layout)
            self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Trajectory")
            return

        s_sim = self.res.s
        n_sim = self.res.n 
        
        # Interpolace X, Y reference na mřížku solveru
        x_ref = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_ref = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.y)
        
        # Výpočet heading reference
        dx = np.gradient(x_ref)
        dy = np.gradient(y_ref)
        psi_ref = np.arctan2(dy, dx)
        
        # Rekonstrukce pozice auta
        x_car = x_ref - n_sim * np.sin(psi_ref)
        y_car = y_ref + n_sim * np.cos(psi_ref)

        # --- 2. Grafy ---
        
        # A) Lateral Deviation Plot (n)
        plot_dev = pg.PlotWidget(title="Lateral deviation (n)")
        plot_dev.setLabel('left', 'Deviation', units='m')
        plot_dev.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_dev.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        
        # Centerline (0) a Tunel
        plot_dev.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=0, angle=0, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=1, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)))    
        plot_dev.plot(s_sim, n_sim, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2), name="Deviation")

        # B) Steering Angle Plot (Delta)
        plot_steer = pg.PlotWidget(title="Wheel angle")
        plot_steer.setLabel('left', 'Steer', units='deg') # Lepší čitelnost ve stupních
        plot_steer.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_steer.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        
        # Příprava dat pro Delta (Input vs State length fix)
        delta_data = self.res.delta
        s_steer = s_sim
        
        # Delta má obvykle délku N, s_sim má N+1. Ořízneme s_sim.
        if len(delta_data) < len(s_steer):
            s_steer = s_sim[:len(delta_data)]
            
        # Převod na stupně
        delta_deg = np.degrees(delta_data)
        
        # Vykreslení
        plot_steer.plot(s_steer, delta_deg, pen=pg.mkPen('r', width=2), name="Steering")
        plot_steer.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=0, angle=0, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=1, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine)))

        # C) XY Map Comparison
        plot_map = pg.PlotWidget(title="Trajectory comparison")
        plot_map.setAspectLocked(True)
        plot_map.setLabel('bottom', 'X', units='m')
        plot_map.setLabel('left', 'Y', units='m')
        plot_map.addLegend()

        plot_map.plot(x_ref, y_ref, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=1, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine), name="Track Center")
        plot_map.plot(x_car, y_car, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2), name="Car Path")
        plot_map.plot([x_car[0]], [y_car[0]], symbol='t1', symbolBrush='g', symbolSize=15, name="Start")

        # --- Layout Assembly ---
        # Stretch faktory určují poměr výšky (1:1:2)
        layout.addWidget(plot_dev, stretch=1)
        layout.addWidget(plot_steer, stretch=1)
        layout.addWidget(plot_map, stretch=2)
        
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Trajectory")

    def init_powertrain_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # --- 1. POWER GRAPH (Zůstává stejný) ---
        v_interp = self.res.v[:-1]
        power_kw = (self.res.f_acc * v_interp) / 1000.0 
        brake_kw = (self.res.f_brake * v_interp) / 1000.0 
        
        plot_p = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power usage")
        plot_p.setLabel('left', 'Power', units='kW')
        plot_p.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_p.addLegend() 
        
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1], power_kw, pen=pg.mkPen('g', width=2), name="Sim")
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1], brake_kw, pen=pg.mkPen('r', width=2), name="Sim")
        plot_p.plot(self.res.s[:-1],-self.res.recuperation/1000,pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2), name="Sim")
        
        # --- PŘÍPRAVA NA ENERGY GRAPH ---
        # Spočítáme čistě trakční energii (bez rekuperace)
        # E = integral(P * dt) -> sum(P * dt)
        # dt = ds / v
        
        # Získáme časové kroky pro simulaci
        dt_sim = np.diff(self.res.t)
        
        # Filtrujeme jen kladný výkon (Trakce)
        # power_kw je výkon na kolech, musíme ho vydělit účinností motoru, abychom dostali spotřebu z baterie
        # self.car.power_eff je např 0.9. Pokud motor dává 90kW, z baterky jde 100kW.
        pos_power_battery = np.maximum(power_kw, 0) / self.car.power_eff 
        
        # Energie v kWh = (kW * s) / 3600
        e_step_sim = (pos_power_battery * dt_sim) / 3600.0
        e_cum_sim = np.cumsum(e_step_sim)
        # Přidáme nulu na začátek, aby délka odpovídala s[:-1] nebo s
        e_cum_sim = np.insert(e_cum_sim, 0, 0.0)

        # --- VALIDATION DATA PROCESSING ---
        val_data = None
        p_ref_kw = None
        e_cum_real = None
        
        if self.track.ref_data is not None:
            val_data = self.calculate_validation_metrics(self.res, self.track.ref_data)
            
            if val_data and val_data.get('p_ref') is not None:
                # Real Power
                p_ref_kw = val_data['p_ref'] / 1000.0 # W -> kW
                plot_p.plot(val_data['s'], p_ref_kw, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DotLine), name="Real Power")
                
                # Real Energy (Traction Only)
                # Musíme odhadnout dt pro realitu
                # Validation metrics nám vrací 'v_ref' interpolované na s_sim
                # Takže můžeme použít stejné dt_sim (protože jsme na stejné dráze)
                # NEBO přesněji: dt = ds / v_ref
                
                ds = np.diff(val_data['s'])
                v_ref_safe = np.maximum(val_data['v_ref'][:-1], 1.0)
                dt_ref = ds / v_ref_safe
               
                pos_power_real = np.maximum(p_ref_kw[:-1], 0)
                
                e_step_real = (pos_power_real * dt_ref) / 3600.0
                e_cum_real = np.cumsum(e_step_real)
                e_cum_real = np.insert(e_cum_real, 0, 0.0)

       
        plot_e = pg.PlotWidget(title="Cumulative energy (discharge only)")
        plot_e.setLabel('left', 'Energy Out', units='kWh')
        plot_e.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_e.addLegend()
        
        # Sim Energy
        # Používáme self.res.s, což má délku N+1, stejně jako e_cum_sim
        plot_e.plot(self.res.s, e_cum_sim, pen=pg.mkPen('orange', width=2), name="Sim")
        
        # Real Energy
        if e_cum_real is not None:
            plot_e.plot(val_data['s'], e_cum_real, pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DotLine), name="Real discharge")
        
        plot_e.setXLink(plot_p)
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Powertrain")

    def init_forces_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        plot_f = pg.PlotWidget(title="Longitudinal Forces")
        plot_f.setLabel('left', 'Force', units='N')
        plot_f.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_f.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        
        plot_f.plot(self.res.s[:-1], self.res.f_acc, pen='g', name="Traction")
        plot_f.plot(self.res.s[:-1], self.res.f_brake, pen='r', name="Braking")
        
        plot_f.addLegend()
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_f)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Forces")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        # Vytvoříme PlotWidget
        self.map_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power map")
        self.map_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        self.map_plot.setLabel('bottom', 'X Position', units='m')
        self.map_plot.setLabel('left', 'Y Position', units='m')


        # --- 1. DATA PREP ---
        s_sim = self.res.s
        # Interpolace souřadnic
        x_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.y)   

        # Výpočet výkonu
        # Zkrátíme o 1 index, aby seděly délky (v_avg je pro intervaly)
        v_avg = self.res.v[:-1]
        f_acc = self.res.f_acc
        f_brake = self.res.f_brake
        
        # Power [kW]
        power_kw = ((f_acc + f_brake) * v_avg) / 1000.0
        
        # X a Y zkrátíme, aby seděly k Power vektoru
        x_plot = x_plot[:-1]
        y_plot = y_plot[:-1]
        
        # Uložíme data do self, abychom k nim mohli v 'mouse_moved'
        self.map_data = {
            'x': x_plot,
            'y': y_plot,
            'p': power_kw,
            'v': v_avg * 3.6, # km/h
            's': s_sim[:-1]
        }

        
        p_min = np.min(power_kw)
        p_max = np.max(power_kw)
        span = p_max - p_min + 1e-9 
        
       
        zero_pos = (0 - p_min) / span
        zero_pos = np.clip(zero_pos, 0.0, 1.0) # Omezit na 0-1

     
        pos = np.array([0.0, zero_pos, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [255, 0, 0, 255],     # Red (Max Regen)
            [0, 150, 255, 255], # Grey/Yellow (0 kW)
            [0, 255, 0, 255]      # Green (Max Power)
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        
        # Vytvoříme ColorMap objekt
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        
        # Získáme barvy pro všechny body najednou (LUT lookup)
        # Nejprve normalizujeme power na 0..1
        norm_power = (power_kw - p_min) / span
        brushes = cmap.map(norm_power, mode='qcolor') # Vrátí pole QColor

     
        self.scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(
            x=x_plot, 
            y=y_plot, 
            size=6,           # Velikost bodu 
            pen=None,         # Bez obrysu
            brush=brushes,    # Naše barvy
            hoverable=True,
            hoverSize=10      # Zvětší se při najetí myší
        )
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.scatter)
        
        # Start marker
        start_marker = pg.ScatterPlotItem(x=[x_plot[0]], y=[y_plot[0]], size=15, symbol='o', brush='w')
        self.map_plot.addItem(start_marker)

 
        self.legend = pg.GradientLegend((20, 150), (10, 10)) # Velikost, Offset
        self.legend.setGradient(cmap.getGradient())
        # Nastavíme popisky (Min, 0, Max)
        labels = {
            f"{p_min:.0f} kW": 0,
            "0 kW": zero_pos,
            f"{p_max:.0f} kW": 1
        }
        self.legend.setLabels(labels)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.legend)

        # --- 5. INTERAKCE (CROSSHAIR & LABEL) ---
        # Přidáme label, který bude ukazovat hodnoty
        self.cursor_label = pg.TextItem(anchor=(0, 1), color="w") # Anchor vlevo dole
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.cursor_label)
        
        # Crosshair (zaměřovač)
        self.v_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=90, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.h_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=0, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.v_line)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.h_line)
        
        # Propojíme pohyb myši s funkcí
        self.map_plot.scene().sigMouseMoved.connect(self.on_map_mouse_move)

        layout.addWidget(self.map_plot)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Power Map")

    def on_map_mouse_move(self, pos):
        """Obsluha pohybu myši nad grafem - hledá nejbližší bod na trati."""
        if self.map_plot.sceneBoundingRect().contains(pos):
            mouse_point = self.map_plot.plotItem.vb.mapSceneToView(pos)
            mx, my = mouse_point.x(), mouse_point.y()
            
            # Najdeme nejbližší bod v datech
            # Vypočteme vzdálenost myši od všech bodů tratě (vektorově - velmi rychlé)
            dists = np.sqrt((self.map_data['x'] - mx)**2 + (self.map_data['y'] - my)**2)
            idx = np.argmin(dists) # Index nejbližšího bodu
            
            # Pokud jsme blízko trati (např. do 20 metrů), zobrazíme info
            if dists[idx] < 20.0:
                x_track = self.map_data['x'][idx]
                y_track = self.map_data['y'][idx]
                p_val = self.map_data['p'][idx]
                v_val = self.map_data['v'][idx]
                s_val = self.map_data['s'][idx]
                
                # Posuneme Crosshair na bod tratě
                self.v_line.setPos(x_track)
                self.h_line.setPos(y_track)
                
                # Barva textu podle výkonu
                color_hex = "#00ff00" if p_val > 0 else "#ff5555" if p_val < 0 else "#ffffff"
                
                # Text Labelu
                html_text = (
                    f"<div style='background-color: rgba(0,0,0,150); padding: 5px; font-size: 14px;'>"
                    f"<b style='color: white;'>Dist: {s_val:.1f} m</b><br>"
                    f"Speed: {v_val:.1f} km/h<br>"
                    f"Power: <b style='color: {color_hex};'>{p_val:.1f} kW</b>"
                    f"</div>"
                )
                
                self.cursor_label.setHtml(html_text)
                self.cursor_label.setPos(x_track, y_track)
                self.cursor_label.setVisible(True)
                self.v_line.setVisible(True)
                self.h_line.setVisible(True)
            else:
                # Jsme moc daleko od tratě
                self.cursor_label.setVisible(False)
                self.v_line.setVisible(False)
                self.h_line.setVisible(False)

    def init_sensitivity_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
       
        if not self.res.sens:
            main_layout.addWidget(QLabel("No sensitivity data available."))
            widget.setLayout(main_layout)
            self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Sensitivity")
            return

        raw = self.res.sens
        
        self.scenario_data = {
            'Mass (m)': {
                'val': self.car.mass, 
                'sens': raw.get('dTime_dm', 0.0), 
                'unit': 'kg'
            },
            'Drag (Cd)': {
                'val': self.car.cd,
                'sens': raw.get('dTime_dCdA', 0.0), 
                'unit': '-'
            },
            'Downforce (Cl)': {
                'val': self.car.cl,
                'sens': raw.get('dTime_dCL', 0.0), 
                'unit': '-'
            }
        }

        # 2. control panel
        control_group = QGroupBox("Scenario Configuration (Multi-Parametric Analysis)")
        grid = QGridLayout()
        grid.setSpacing(15) 
        
        # Hlavičky tabulky
        grid.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Parameter</b>"), 0, 0)
        grid.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Base Value</b>"), 0, 1)
        grid.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Change (%)</b>"), 0, 2)
        grid.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Delta Time</b>"), 0, 3) 

        self.inputs = {}        # reference na spinboxy
        self.deltas_labels = {} # reference na labely s výsledky

        row = 1
        for name, data in self.scenario_data.items():
            # A) name of parameter
            lbl_name = QLabel(name)
            lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-size: 14px;")
            
            # B) value
            lbl_val = QLabel(f"{data['val']:.3f} {data['unit']}")
            lbl_val.setStyleSheet("color: #666; font-family: Consolas;")
            
            # C) Input SpinBox (%)
            spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin.setRange(-50.0, 50.0)
            spin.setValue(0.0)
            spin.setSuffix("%")
            spin.setSingleStep(0.5)
            spin.setFixedWidth(100)
            spin.valueChanged.connect(self.update_scenario) # Live update
            
            self.inputs[name] = spin
            
            # D) Delta Label 
            lbl_delta = QLabel("0.000 s")
            lbl_delta.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight)
            lbl_delta.setStyleSheet("font-family: Consolas; font-weight: bold;")
            self.deltas_labels[name] = lbl_delta

            # grid import
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_val, row, 1)
            grid.addWidget(spin, row, 2)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_delta, row, 3)
            row += 1

        # Reset button
        btn_reset = QPushButton("Reset All to 0%")
        btn_reset.clicked.connect(self.reset_scenario)
        grid.addWidget(btn_reset, row, 2, 1, 2)

        control_group.setLayout(grid)
        main_layout.addWidget(control_group)

        # 3. results panel
        self.lbl_scenario_results = QLabel()
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setTextFormat(Qt.TextFormat.RichText)
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setStyleSheet("""
            background-color: #f8f9fa; 
            border: 1px solid #ddd; 
            border-radius: 5px;
            padding: 20px; 
        """)
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_scenario_results)
        main_layout.addStretch() 
        
        widget.setLayout(main_layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Sensitivity")
        
  
        self.update_scenario()


    def reset_scenario(self):
        """resets all spinboxs to 0"""
        for spin in self.inputs.values():
            spin.blockSignals(True) 
            spin.setValue(0.0)
            spin.blockSignals(False)
        self.update_scenario()

    def update_scenario(self):
        
        total_dt = 0.0
        
        # iteration over parameters
        for name, spin in self.inputs.items():
            pct = spin.value()
            data = self.scenario_data[name]
            
            # change
            # Delta Param = Base * (Pct / 100)
            d_param = data['val'] * (pct / 100.0)
            
            # Delta Time = Sens * Delta Param
            d_time = data['sens'] * d_param
            
            total_dt += d_time
            
            # color gang
            color = "#28a745" if d_time < 0 else "#dc3545" if d_time > 0 else "#888"
            sign = "+" if d_time > 0 else ""
            self.deltas_labels[name].setText(f"<span style='color:{color}'>{sign}{d_time:.4f} s</span>")

        # overal sum
        base_time = self.res.lap_time
        new_time = base_time + total_dt
        
        # color
        diff_color = "#28a745" if total_dt < 0 else "#dc3545" if total_dt > 0 else "#333"
        diff_sign = "+" if total_dt > 0 else ""

        # 3. HTML 
        html = f"""
        <div style='text-align: center; font-family: sans-serif;'>
            <h3 style='margin-bottom: 5px; color: #666; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px;'>Predicted Lap Time</h3>
            
            <div style='font-size: 48px; font-weight: bold; color: {diff_color}; margin: 10px 0;'>
                {new_time:.3f} s
            </div>
            
            <div style='font-size: 20px; color: {diff_color}; font-weight: bold;'>
                ({diff_sign}{total_dt:.3f} s)
            </div>
            
            <div style='margin-top: 15px; font-size: 14px; color: #999; border-top: 1px solid #eee; padding-top: 10px; display: inline-block;'>
                Baseline: {base_time:.3f} s
            </div>
        </div>
        """
        
        self.lbl_scenario_results.setText(html)

    def init_drs_map_tab(self):
        
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.drs_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="DRS Usage Map")
        self.drs_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        self.drs_plot.setLabel('bottom', 'X Position', units='m')
        self.drs_plot.setLabel('left', 'Y Position', units='m')

        # --- DATA PREP ---
        # DRS usage je pole 0..1
        drs_data = self.res.drs_usage
        
        # Interpolace souřadnic na délku DRS pole (které má délku N)
        # res.s má délku N+1, takže použijeme s[:-1]
        s_sim = self.res.s[:-1]
        
        x_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_plot = np.interp(s_sim, self.track.s, self.track.y) 

        # --- COLOR MAPPING ---
        # 0.0 (Closed) -> Red
        # 1.0 (Open)   -> Green
        # Mezi tím -> Interpolace
        
        pos = np.array([0.0, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [255, 0, 0, 255],   # Red (Closed)
            [0, 255, 0, 255]    # Green (Open)
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        
        # Obarvení bodů
        brushes = cmap.map(drs_data, mode='qcolor')

        # --- SCATTER PLOT ---
        self.drs_scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(
            x=x_plot, 
            y=y_plot, 
            size=6, 
            pen=None, 
            brush=brushes,
            hoverable=True,
            hoverSize=10
        )
        self.drs_plot.addItem(self.drs_scatter)
        
        # Start/Finish line marker
        self.drs_plot.plot([x_plot[0]], [y_plot[0]], symbol='o', symbolBrush='w', symbolSize=10)

        # --- LEGEND ---
        legend = pg.GradientLegend((20, 100), (10, 10))
        legend.setGradient(cmap.getGradient())
        legend.setLabels({"Closed": 0, "Open": 1})
        self.drs_plot.addItem(legend)

        layout.addWidget(self.drs_plot)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "DRS Map")

    def init_soc_tab(self):
        """Initializes the State of Charge (SoC) tab if data is available."""
        # Check if soc exists and is not None
        if self.res.SoC is None:
            return

        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # Create PlotWidget
        plot_soc = pg.PlotWidget(title="State of charge (SoC)")
        plot_soc.setLabel('left', 'SoC', units='%')
        plot_soc.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        plot_soc.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        
        # Ensure lengths match (s is usually N+1, soc might be N or N+1)
        s_plot = self.res.s[:len(self.res.SoC)]
        
        # Plot the data
        plot_soc.plot(s_plot, self.res.SoC, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2), name="SoC")
        
        layout.addWidget(plot_soc)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Battery")

--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\ui\results_window.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\sensitivity_plot.py ---
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QTabWidget
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qtagg import FigureCanvasQTAgg, NavigationToolbar2QT
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
import numpy as np
from scipy.interpolate import griddata, RegularGridInterpolator
import pyqtgraph as pg
from PyQt6.QtGui import QColor  
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt

class Universal3DWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, x_grid, y_grid, z_grid, x_label, y_label, z_label, title, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
       
        self.x_label = x_label
        self.y_label = y_label
        self.z_label = z_label
        self.plot_title = title
        
        # Layout
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.setLayout(layout)
        
        # Matplotlib figure
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(10, 8), dpi=100)
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
        self.toolbar = NavigationToolbar2QT(self.canvas, self)
        
        layout.addWidget(self.toolbar)
        layout.addWidget(self.canvas)
        

        self.plot_3d(x_grid, y_grid, z_grid)

    def plot_3d(self, X_raw, Y_raw, Z_raw):
        self.fig.clear()
        
        ax3d = self.fig.add_subplot(1, 1, 1, projection='3d')
        
        x_flat = X_raw.flatten()
        y_flat = Y_raw.flatten()
        z_flat = Z_raw.flatten()
        
        mask = ~np.isnan(z_flat)
        x_valid = x_flat[mask]
        y_valid = y_flat[mask]
        z_valid = z_flat[mask]
        
        if len(z_valid) < 4:
            print("Not enough points for interpolation")
            return

        resolution = 100 
        xi = np.linspace(min(x_valid), max(x_valid), resolution)
        yi = np.linspace(min(y_valid), max(y_valid), resolution)
        XI, YI = np.meshgrid(xi, yi)
        
        # Cubic interpolation
        ZI = griddata((x_valid, y_valid), z_valid, (XI, YI), method='cubic')
        
        # Plot 3D Surface
        surf = ax3d.plot_surface(XI, YI, ZI, 
                               cmap='viridis', edgecolor='none', 
                               linewidth=0, antialiased=True, alpha=0.9,
                               rcount=resolution, ccount=resolution)
        
      
        ax3d.set_xlabel(self.x_label)
        ax3d.set_ylabel(self.y_label)
        ax3d.set_zlabel(self.z_label)
        ax3d.set_title(self.plot_title)
        
        z_min = np.nanmin(ZI)
        ax3d.contourf(XI, YI, ZI, zdir='z', offset=z_min, cmap='viridis', alpha=0.3)
        ax3d.set_zlim(z_min, np.nanmax(ZI))

        self.fig.colorbar(surf, ax=ax3d, shrink=0.5, aspect=10, label=self.z_label)
        ax3d.view_init(elev=25, azim=-135)

        self.fig.tight_layout()
        self.canvas.draw()


class LaptimeSensitivityPlot(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, results, laps):
        super().__init__()
        self.setWindowTitle("Laptime sensitivity results")
        self.resize(1200, 900)
        
        self.all_results = results

        self.results = [r for r in results if getattr(r, 'meta_soc', 0) and abs(r.meta_soc - 1.0) < 1e-5]
        self.laps = laps
        
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.setLayout(main_layout)
        
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    def open_windows(self):
        if not self.results:
            print("Warning: No results provided to LaptimeSensitivityPlot.")
            return

        acp_widget = self.create_acp_plot()
        lt_widget = self.create_laptimes_energy()
        opt_cap_3d_widget = self.create_optimal_capacity_3d()
        opt_cap_2d_widget = self.create_optimal_capacity_2d() 
        soc_widget = self.create_soc_vs_laptime_plot()

       
        self.tabs.addTab(acp_widget, "Laptime map")
        self.tabs.addTab(lt_widget, "Energy vs points")
        self.tabs.addTab(opt_cap_3d_widget, "Optimal capacity map")
        self.tabs.addTab(opt_cap_2d_widget, "Optimal capacity slices") 
        self.tabs.addTab(soc_widget,"SoC vs laptime")
        
        self.show()

    def get_laptime_functions(self):
        x_vals = sorted(list(set([r.meta_energy*self.laps for r in self.results]))) 
        y_vals = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.results])))  
        
        nx = len(x_vals)
        ny = len(y_vals)
        
        Z_time = np.zeros((nx, ny))
        Z_eff = np.zeros((nx, ny))
        Z_time[:] = np.nan
        Z_eff[:] = np.nan
        
        x_map = {val: i for i, val in enumerate(x_vals)}
        y_map = {val: i for i, val in enumerate(y_vals)}
        
        for r in self.results:
            ix = x_map.get(r.meta_energy*self.laps)
            iy = y_map.get(r.meta_regen)
            if ix is not None and iy is not None:
                t = r.lap_time
                Z_time[ix, iy] = t
                Z_eff[ix, iy] = (t**2) * (r.meta_energy*self.laps)
        
        if np.isnan(Z_time).any():
            Z_time = np.nan_to_num(Z_time, nan=np.nanmax(Z_time))
            Z_eff = np.nan_to_num(Z_eff, nan=np.nanmax(Z_eff))

        interp_method = 'cubic' if (nx >= 4 and ny >= 4) else 'linear'

        interp_time_obj = RegularGridInterpolator((x_vals, y_vals), Z_time, method=interp_method, bounds_error=False, fill_value=None)
        
        def laptime_func(E_target, Regen):
            E_arr = np.atleast_1d(E_target)
            R_arr = np.atleast_1d(Regen)
            if E_arr.shape != R_arr.shape:
                E_arr, R_arr = np.broadcast_arrays(E_arr, R_arr)
                
            pts = np.column_stack((E_arr.ravel(), R_arr.ravel()))
            res = interp_time_obj(pts).reshape(E_arr.shape)
            return float(res) if res.size == 1 else res

        return laptime_func, None

    def create_acp_plot(self):
        x_vals = sorted(list(set([r.meta_energy*self.laps for r in self.results])))
        y_vals = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.results])))
        
        nx = len(x_vals)
        ny = len(y_vals)
        X, Y = np.meshgrid(x_vals, y_vals)
        Z = np.zeros((ny, nx))
        Z[:] = np.nan
        
        x_map = {val: i for i, val in enumerate(x_vals)}
        y_map = {val: i for i, val in enumerate(y_vals)}
        
        for r in self.results:
            ix = x_map.get(r.meta_energy*self.laps)
            iy = y_map.get(r.meta_regen)
            if ix is not None and iy is not None:
                Z[iy, ix] = r.lap_time

        return Universal3DWindow(X, Y, Z, 
                                 x_label='Total energy target [kWh]', 
                                 y_label='Regen percentage [%]', 
                                 z_label='Lap Time [s]', 
                                 title='Lap time sensitivity')

    def create_laptimes_energy(self):
        laptime_func, _ = self.get_laptime_functions()
        
        all_times = np.array([r.lap_time for r in self.results])
        all_energies = np.array([r.meta_energy*self.laps for r in self.results])
        all_eff_factors = (all_times**2) * all_energies
        
        T_min_global = np.min(all_times) * (1 - 0.07)
        Best_Eff_global = np.min(all_eff_factors)
        
        lt_widget = pg.GraphicsLayoutWidget(title="Energy vs laptime and points")
        lt_widget.setBackground('w')
        
        p1 = lt_widget.addPlot(title="Lap Time [s]")
        p1.setLabel('left', "Time", units='s')
        p1.addLegend(offset=(30, 30))

        lt_widget.nextRow()

        p2 = lt_widget.addPlot(title="Total points vs energy used")
        p2.setLabel('left', "Points", units='pts')
        p2.setLabel('bottom', "Total Energy target for endurance", units='kWh')
        p2.setXLink(p1)
        p2.addLegend(offset=(30, 30))
        p2.plot([0], [0], pen=pg.mkPen('k', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine), name="Total Score")

        min_e, max_e = np.min(all_energies), np.max(all_energies)
        regens_set = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.results])))
        min_r, max_r = min(regens_set), max(regens_set)
        
        x_smooth = np.linspace(min_e, max_e, 200)
        regen_levels = np.linspace(min_r, max_r, 4) 
        
        global_max_points = -1
        global_opt_energy = 0
        
        COLORS = [QColor(0, 114, 178), QColor(213, 94, 0), QColor(0, 158, 115), QColor(204, 121, 167)]
        
        for i, r_val in enumerate(regen_levels):
            y_time = laptime_func(x_smooth, np.full_like(x_smooth, r_val))
            y_eff_factor = (y_time**2) * x_smooth
            
            pts_time = np.zeros_like(y_time)
            T_max = T_min_global * 1.5
            mask_t = y_time <= T_max
            pts_time[mask_t] = (250 - 25) * ((T_max - y_time[mask_t]) / (T_max - T_min_global))**2 + 25
            
            pts_eff = np.zeros_like(y_eff_factor)
            Ef_max = (Best_Eff_global * 2) * 1.2
            mask_e = y_eff_factor <= Ef_max
            pts_eff[mask_e] = 75 * ((Ef_max - y_eff_factor[mask_e]) / (Ef_max - Best_Eff_global))**2
            
            pts_total = pts_time + pts_eff
            
            local_idx = np.argmax(pts_total)
            local_opt_energy = x_smooth[local_idx]
            local_max_points = pts_total[local_idx]
            
            if local_max_points > global_max_points:
                global_max_points = local_max_points
                global_opt_energy = local_opt_energy
            
            base_color = COLORS[i % len(COLORS)]
            label_text = f"Regen {r_val*100:.0f}%"
            
            p1.plot(x_smooth, y_time, pen=pg.mkPen(color=base_color, width=3), name=label_text)
            p2.plot(x_smooth, pts_total, pen=pg.mkPen(color=base_color, width=3, style=Qt.PenStyle.SolidLine))
            
            p2.plot([local_opt_energy], [local_max_points], symbol='o', symbolSize=10, 
                    symbolBrush=base_color, symbolPen='k', name=None)

        v_line = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=global_opt_energy, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashDotLine))
        p2.addItem(v_line)
        p1.addItem(pg.InfiniteLine(pos=global_opt_energy, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashDotLine)))

        info_html = (f"<div style='color:black; background-color: rgba(220,220,255,0.85); "
                     f"padding:5px; border:2px solid blue; font-weight:bold;'>"
                     f"Global Max Points: {global_max_points:.1f} pts<br>"
                     f"Opt Total Energy: {global_opt_energy:.2f} kWh </div>")
        
        text = pg.TextItem(html=info_html, anchor=(0.5, 0))
        text.setPos(global_opt_energy, global_max_points)
        p2.addItem(text)
        
        return lt_widget

    def create_optimal_capacity_3d(self):
        laptime_func, _ = self.get_laptime_functions()
        
        all_times = np.array([r.lap_time for r in self.results])
        all_energies = np.array([r.meta_energy*self.laps for r in self.results])
        all_eff_factors = (all_times**2) * all_energies
        
        base_t_min = np.min(all_times)
        best_eff_glob = np.min(all_eff_factors)
        
        regens_set = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.results])))
        min_r, max_r = min(regens_set), max(regens_set)
        min_e, max_e = np.min(all_energies), np.max(all_energies)
        
        regen_grid = np.linspace(min_r, max_r, 40)
        offset_grid = np.linspace(0.0, 25.0, 40) 
        
        X_regen, Y_offset = np.meshgrid(regen_grid, offset_grid)
        Z_opt_cap = np.zeros_like(X_regen)
        
        e_test = np.linspace(min_e, max_e, 200)
        
        for i in range(X_regen.shape[0]):
            for j in range(X_regen.shape[1]):
                r_val = X_regen[i, j]
                offset_pct = Y_offset[i, j]
                
                t_min_glob = base_t_min * (1.0 - (offset_pct / 100.0))
                
                y_time = laptime_func(e_test, np.full_like(e_test, r_val))
                y_eff_factor = (y_time**2) * e_test
                
                pts_time = np.zeros_like(y_time)
                T_max = t_min_glob * 1.5
                mask_t = y_time <= T_max
                if np.any(mask_t):
                    pts_time[mask_t] = (250 - 25) * ((T_max - y_time[mask_t]) / (T_max - t_min_glob))**2 + 25
                
                pts_eff = np.zeros_like(y_eff_factor)
                Ef_max = (best_eff_glob * 2) * 1.2
                mask_e = y_eff_factor <= Ef_max
                if np.any(mask_e):
                    pts_eff[mask_e] = 75 * ((Ef_max - y_eff_factor[mask_e]) / (Ef_max - best_eff_glob))**2
                
                pts_total = pts_time + pts_eff
                best_idx = np.argmax(pts_total)
                Z_opt_cap[i, j] = e_test[best_idx]

        return Universal3DWindow(
            X_regen, Y_offset, Z_opt_cap, 
            x_label='Regen percentage [%]', 
            y_label='T_min Offset [%]', 
            z_label='Optimal Capacity [kWh]', 
            title='Optimal Capacity vs Regen & Time Offset'
        )

    
    def create_optimal_capacity_2d(self):
        """Vygeneruje 2D plot: Osa X = Regen, Osa Y = Optimal Capacity pro různé Time Offsets."""
        laptime_func, _ = self.get_laptime_functions()
        
        all_times = np.array([r.lap_time for r in self.results])
        all_energies = np.array([r.meta_energy*self.laps for r in self.results])
        all_eff_factors = (all_times**2) * all_energies
        
        base_t_min = np.min(all_times)
        best_eff_glob = np.min(all_eff_factors)
        
        regens_set = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.results])))
        min_r, max_r = min(regens_set), max(regens_set)
        min_e, max_e = np.min(all_energies), np.max(all_energies)
        
        regen_test = np.linspace(min_r, max_r, 50)
        e_test = np.linspace(min_e, max_e, 200)
        
        # Testované offsety (0% až 25% pomalejší konkurence)
        offsets = [0.0, 5.0, 10.0, 15.0, 20.0, 25.0]
        
        plot_widget = pg.PlotWidget(title="Optimal capacity vs regen for different times")
        plot_widget.setBackground('w')
        plot_widget.setLabel('bottom', "Regen percentage", units="%")
        plot_widget.setLabel('left', "Optimal capacity", units="kWh")
        plot_widget.addLegend(offset=(30, 30))
        plot_widget.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        
        for i, offset_pct in enumerate(offsets):
            opt_caps = []
            
            
            t_min_glob = base_t_min * (1.0 - (offset_pct / 100.0))
            T_max = t_min_glob * 1.5
            Ef_max = (best_eff_glob * 2) * 1.2
            
            for r_val in regen_test:
                y_time = laptime_func(e_test, np.full_like(e_test, r_val))
                y_eff_factor = (y_time**2) * e_test
                
                pts_time = np.zeros_like(y_time)
                mask_t = y_time <= T_max
                if np.any(mask_t):
                    pts_time[mask_t] = (250 - 25) * ((T_max - y_time[mask_t]) / (T_max - t_min_glob))**2 + 25
                
                pts_eff = np.zeros_like(y_eff_factor)
                mask_e = y_eff_factor <= Ef_max
                if np.any(mask_e):
                    pts_eff[mask_e] = 75 * ((Ef_max - y_eff_factor[mask_e]) / (Ef_max - best_eff_glob))**2
                
                pts_total = pts_time + pts_eff
                best_idx = np.argmax(pts_total)
                opt_caps.append(e_test[best_idx])
            
            # Vygenerování unikátní barvy pro každý offset
            color = pg.intColor(i, hues=len(offsets))
            
           
            plot_widget.plot(regen_test * 100, opt_caps, pen=pg.mkPen(color=color, width=3), name=f"Time offset {offset_pct:.0f}%")
            
        return plot_widget
    
    def create_soc_vs_laptime_plot(self):
        # 1. Zjistíme unikátní hodnoty z celého datasetu
        e_targets = sorted(list(set([r.meta_energy for r in self.all_results])))
        regens = sorted(list(set([r.meta_regen for r in self.all_results])))
        socs = sorted(list(set([r.meta_soc for r in self.all_results])))
        
        if not e_targets or not regens or not socs:
            return QWidget()
            
        # 2. Vybereme fixní hodnoty (prostřední E_target a maximální Regen)
        fixed_e_target = e_targets[len(e_targets) // 2]
        fixed_regen = regens[-1]
        
        # 3. Vyfiltrujeme data
        plot_data = []
        for r in self.all_results:
            # Porovnáváme floaty s tolerancí (abs < 1e-5)
            if abs(r.meta_energy - fixed_e_target) < 1e-5 and abs(r.meta_regen - fixed_regen) < 1e-5:
                plot_data.append((r.meta_soc, r.lap_time))
                
        # Seřadíme podle SoC (od nejmenšího po největší)
        plot_data.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        
        x_vals = [d[0] * 100 for d in plot_data] # Převod SoC na procenta (Osa X)
        y_vals = [d[1] for d in plot_data]       # Laptime (Osa Y)
        
        # 4. Vytvoření PyQtGraph widgetu
        title = f"SoC vs Laptime (Fixed Target = {fixed_e_target*self.laps:.2f} kWh, Regen = {fixed_regen*100:.0f}%)"
        plot_widget = pg.PlotWidget(title=title)
        plot_widget.setBackground('w')
        
        plot_widget.setLabel('bottom', "State of Charge (SoC)", units="%")
        plot_widget.setLabel('left', "Lap Time", units="s")
        plot_widget.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.3)
        
        # 5. Vykreslení čáry a bodů
        line_pen = pg.mkPen(color=QColor(213, 94, 0), width=3) # Oranžová čára
        plot_widget.plot(x_vals, y_vals, pen=line_pen, symbol='o', symbolSize=10, 
                         symbolBrush=QColor(0, 114, 178), symbolPen='k') # Modré body s černým okrajem
        
        # 6. Invertování osy X (aby 100% bylo vlevo a 0% vpravo, jak se baterie vybíjí)
        plot_widget.getViewBox().invertX(True)
        
        return plot_widget

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\utils\drs_69.py ---
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# parametry
kappa_k = 150.0
kappa_thr = 0.1
kappa_max = 0.2

# vstupní zakřivení
kappa_vals = np.linspace(0, 0.2, 200)
kappa_vals = kappa_vals/kappa_max

# drs_curve funkce (ekvivalent CasADi)
drs_vals = 1 / (1 + np.exp(kappa_k * (np.abs(kappa_vals) - kappa_thr)))

# plot
plt.figure(figsize=(6,4))
plt.plot(kappa_vals, drs_vals, label='DRS vs Curvature')
plt.axvline(kappa_thr, color='r', linestyle='--', label='Threshold')
plt.xlabel('Curvature κ [1/m]')
plt.ylabel('DRS factor')
plt.title('Soft DRS curve vs curvature')
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()
plt.show()

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\utils\regen_model.py ---
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# ---------------------------
# Simulation parameters
# ---------------------------
N_SIM = 50     # number of Monte Carlo runs
N_LAPS = 22     # laps per run
REGEN_INIT = 0.94

BASE_DRIFT = -0.0015
SIGMA = 0.025


# ---------------------------
# Regen update function
# ---------------------------
def update_regen_usage(regen_eff, base_drift=-0.001, sigma=0.01):
    
    
    noise = np.random.normal(0.0, sigma)
    regen_eff += base_drift + noise
    regen_eff = np.clip(regen_eff, 0.5, 1.0)

    return regen_eff

# ---------------------------
# Run Monte Carlo simulations
# ---------------------------
all_runs = np.zeros((N_SIM, N_LAPS))

for sim in range(N_SIM):
    regen_eff = REGEN_INIT

    for lap in range(N_LAPS):

        

        regen_eff = update_regen_usage(
            regen_eff,
            BASE_DRIFT,
            SIGMA
        )

        all_runs[sim, lap] = regen_eff

# ---------------------------
# Compute statistics
# ---------------------------
mean_curve = np.mean(all_runs, axis=0)
std_curve = np.std(all_runs, axis=0)

# ---------------------------
# Plot
# ---------------------------
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))

# individual runs
for i in range(N_SIM):
    plt.plot(all_runs[i], alpha=0.3)

# mean
plt.plot(mean_curve, linewidth=3, label="Mean regen_eff")


plt.title("Monte Carlo: Regen Efficiency Drift Over Laps")
plt.xlabel("Lap")
plt.ylabel("regen_eff")
plt.ylim(0.45, 1.02)
plt.grid(True)
plt.legend()

plt.show()

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\utils\saver.py ---
import numpy as np
import os
from scipy.interpolate import interp1d

def save_results_to_npz(filepath, res, track, ds):
    
    
    #results folder has to exist
    directory = os.path.dirname(filepath)
    if directory and not os.path.exists(directory):
        os.makedirs(directory)

    sens_array = np.array([res.sens]) if res.sens else np.array([])


    print(f"Saving results to: {filepath}")

    f_x = interp1d(track.s, track.x, kind='linear', fill_value="extrapolate")
    f_y = interp1d(track.s, track.y, kind='linear', fill_value="extrapolate")

    x_aligned = f_x(res.s)
    y_aligned = f_y(res.s)
    
    np.savez_compressed(
        filepath,
        #main data
        t=res.t,
        v=res.v,
        s=res.s,
        x= x_aligned,
        y= y_aligned,
        E=res.E,
        f_acc=res.f_acc,
        f_brake=res.f_brake,
        
        # scalar values
        lap_time=res.lap_time,
        
        #metadata
        track_name=str(track.name),
        ds=float(ds),
        
        # sensitivity dictionary
        sens=sens_array
    )

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\utils\tire_evolution.py ---
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

# ---------------------------
# CONFIG
# ---------------------------
Total_Laps = 22
Num_Simulations = 50

Base_Mu_X = 1.3  # target optimal grip

Tire_Model = {
    "Cold_Factor": 0.6,   # start grip fraction
    "Warmup_Tau": 1.5,    # warmup time constant
    "Noise_Std": 0.016     # driver inconsistency
}

# ---------------------------
# PREP
# ---------------------------
laps_vector = np.arange(1, Total_Laps + 1)

mu_optimal = Base_Mu_X
mu_cold = Base_Mu_X * Tire_Model["Cold_Factor"]

plt.figure(figsize=(10, 6))
plt.grid(True)

# ---------------------------
# MONTE CARLO SIM
# ---------------------------
for sim in range(Num_Simulations):
    grip_history = np.zeros(Total_Laps)

    for lap in range(Total_Laps):
        # Exponential warmup curve
        warmup_progress = 1 - np.exp(-(lap + 1) / Tire_Model["Warmup_Tau"])

        current_base_mu = mu_cold + (mu_optimal - mu_cold) * warmup_progress

        # Noise
        noise = Tire_Model["Noise_Std"] * np.random.randn()

        mu_lap = current_base_mu + noise

        # Clip grip lower bound
        min_mu = mu_cold * 0.9
        mu_lap = max(mu_lap, min_mu)

        grip_history[lap] = mu_lap

    # Plot each simulation
    plt.plot(laps_vector, grip_history, color=(0.2, 0.6, 1.0, 0.3), linewidth=1.5)

# ---------------------------
# IDEAL CURVE (NO NOISE)
# ---------------------------
ideal_curve = mu_cold + (mu_optimal - mu_cold) * (1 - np.exp(-laps_vector / Tire_Model["Warmup_Tau"]))
plt.plot(laps_vector, ideal_curve, "r--", linewidth=2, label="Ideal warmup")

plt.axhline(Base_Mu_X, color="black", linestyle="-", label="Optimal Grip")

# ---------------------------
# PLOT STYLING
# ---------------------------
plt.title(
    f"Tire evolution model (Start: {Tire_Model['Cold_Factor']*100:.0f}%, Tau: {Tire_Model['Warmup_Tau']:.1f})"
)
plt.xlabel("Lap")
plt.ylabel("Longitudinal Friction (μx)")
#plt.ylim([Base_Mu_X * 0.8, Base_Mu_X * 1.05])
plt.legend()

plt.show()

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: architecure.md ---
# Laptime & Strategy Optimizer 🏎️

Tento projekt je desktopová aplikace napsaná v Pythonu (PyQt5) určená pro pokročilou simulaci časů na kolo a optimalizaci závodní strategie (endurance). Aplikace využívá asynchronní výpočty přes worker vlákna, aby uživatelské rozhraní zůstalo plynulé i při náročných optimalizačních úlohách.

## 🚀 Hlavní funkce

* **Fyzikální modely:** Definice vozidla (`Car`) a tratě (`Track`), výpočet odporu vzduchu, limitů adheze a rekuperace.
* **Solvery (Optimalizace):**
    * `MassPointSolver` a `SingleTrackSolver` pro výpočet ideální stopy a času na kolo.
    * `StrategyMaker` pro optimalizaci spotřeby energie v čase (Endurance).
* **Analýza a UI:** Porovnávání výsledků (`CompareWindow`), analýza DRS, Monte Carlo simulace zahřívání pneumatik a interaktivní grafy.
* **Asynchronní zpracování:** Oddělení těžkých výpočtů od GUI pomocí `QThread` (`SolverWorker`).

## 🏗️ Architektura projektu

Aplikace je rozdělena do logických vrstev. Níže je zjednodušený diagram tříd jádra aplikace a výpočtů:

```mermaid
classDiagram
    class SolverResults {
        <<dataclass>>
        +bool success
        +float lap_time
        +ndarray E
        +ndarray SoC
    }

    class Car {
        +float mass
        +get_aero_drag()
        +get_recuperation_limit()
    }

    class Track {
        +str name
        +get_curvature()
    }

    class SolverWorker {
        <<QThread>>
        +run()
        +run_endurance_opt()
    }

    class StrategyMaker {
        +solve()
    }

    SolverWorker o-- Car : holds
    SolverWorker o-- Track : holds
    SolverWorker o-- StrategyMaker : uses
    StrategyMaker ..> SolverResults : creates
--- KONEC SOUBORU: architecure.md ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: pack.py ---
import os

#script for vibe coding, packs the entire project into txt which can be inserted into gemini or chat gpt for the ai to understand the context


PROJECT_DIR = "." 
OUTPUT_FILE = "project_context.txt" 

IGNORE_DIRS = {'.git', 'venv', 'env', '__pycache__', '.idea', '.vscode', 'build', 'dist'}


ALLOWED_EXTS = {'.py', '.txt', '.md', '.json', '.ini', '.yaml'}

def pack_project():
   
    
    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as outfile:
      
        outfile.write("=== STRUKTURA PROJEKTU ===\n")
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_DIR):
          
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for file in files:
                if any(file.endswith(ext) for ext in ALLOWED_EXTS) and file != OUTPUT_FILE:
                    filepath = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(root, file), PROJECT_DIR)
                    outfile.write(f"- {filepath}\n")
        
        outfile.write("\n=== ZDROJOVÉ KÓDY ===\n\n")
        

        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_DIR):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for file in files:
                if any(file.endswith(ext) for ext in ALLOWED_EXTS) and file != OUTPUT_FILE:
                    filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
                    rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, PROJECT_DIR)
                    
                    outfile.write(f"--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: {rel_path} ---\n")
                    try:
                        with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as infile:
                            outfile.write(infile.read() + "\n")
                    except Exception as e:
                        outfile.write(f"// CHYBA PŘI ČTENÍ SOUBORU: {e}\n")
                    outfile.write(f"--- KONEC SOUBORU: {rel_path} ---\n\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\core\car.py ---
from dataclasses import dataclass,field
import numpy as np
import casadi as ca


@dataclass
class Car:
    # --- 1. Parameters from UI ---
    mass: float           # [kg]
    cd: float             # [-] 
    cl: float             # [-]
    mu_x: float           # [-] 
    mu_y: float           # [-] 
    acp_capacity: float
    drs_allowed: bool
    energy_limit: float = None  # [kWh] 
    performance_delta: float = 0.0


    # Calculated fields 
    fz_static_f: float = field(init=False)
    fz_static_r: float = field(init=False)
    cs_static_f : float = field(init=False)
    cs_static_r : float = field(init=False)

    model_type: str = "mass_point"

    # Constants
    rho = 1.225
    g = 9.81

    # Powertrain
    power_max =80000
    brake_max = 60000
    power_eff = 0.88
    regen_eff = 0.0
    fix_drag = 230
    wheel_radius = 0.205
    max_torque = 30.0
    gear_ratio = 11.46
    brake_bias = 0.55
    power_dist = 0.5

    #soc limitations
    soc_grid_power = [0.0, 0.04, 0.08, 0.12, 0.22, 0.32, 0.42, 0.52, 0.70, 1.00,1.1]
    power_limit_W = [10e3, 14e3, 21e3, 35e3, 62e3, 79e3, 89e3, 95e3, 100e3, 100e3,100e3]
    soc_to_power_interp = ca.interpolant('soc_power', 'bspline', [soc_grid_power], power_limit_W)
   
    # Aero
    cd_drs = 1.17
    cl_drs = 4.2
    cop = 0.53

    # Suspension
    cogr = 0.5 
    cogz = 0.3
    sus_t = 0.2
    Iz = 110 
    wheelbase = 1.53 
    weight_distrib = 0.46 # to rear
    steeringAngle_max = 25/180*ca.pi;
    
  
    lf = wheelbase * cogr
    lr = wheelbase - lf
    
    tire_model = { 
        "cold_factor": 0.8, 
        "warmup_tau": 1.6, 
        "noise_std": 0.03, 
        "mu_x_nominal": 1.22,
        "mu_y_nominal":1.22
    }

    def __post_init__(self):
        # calculate static weight
        self.fz_static_f = self.mass * self.g * (1 - self.weight_distrib)
        self.fz_static_r = self.mass * self.g * self.weight_distrib
        #self.cs_static_f = self.get_cornering_stiffness(self.fz_static_f)
        #self.cs_static_r = self.get_cornering_stiffness(self.fz_static_r)
        #print(f"cornering stiffness front: {self.cs_static_f}")
        self.cs_static_f = 2 * self.get_cornering_stiffness(self.fz_static_f/2)
        self.cs_static_r = 2 * self.get_cornering_stiffness(self.fz_static_r/2)

    @classmethod
    def from_ui_widget(cls, ui):
        """
        Creates car instance from UI widget (MainWindow).
        """
       
        if ui.check_energy_limit.isChecked():
            e_lim = ui.input_limit_e.value()
        else:
            e_lim = None 

        model_data = ui.combo_model.currentData()
        
        return cls(
            mass = ui.input_mass.value(),
            cd = ui.input_cd.value(),
            cl = ui.input_cl.value(),
            mu_x = ui.input_mu_x.value(),
            mu_y = ui.input_mu_y.value(),
            energy_limit = e_lim,
            acp_capacity = ui.input_battery_cap.value(),
            drs_allowed = ui.check_drs.isChecked(),
            model_type = model_data
        )
    

    # car calculation functions 
    def get_aero_drag(self, v, cd = None):
        #if we pass cd as optimization parameter
        use_cd = cd if cd is not None else self.cd
        return self.fix_drag + 0.5 * self.rho * use_cd * v**2
    

    def get_recuperation_limit(self, SoC_ca):
        """
        Vrací spojitý (hladký) limit rekuperace ve Wattech pro dané SoC.
        """
        P_max = 40e3   # 40 kW limit
        soc_mid = 0.885 # Kde křivka protíná 20 kW
        k = 120.0       # Strmost poklesu
        
        return P_max / (1.0 + ca.exp(k * (SoC_ca - soc_mid)))
    
    def tire_fy(self, alpha, Fz):
            """
            Linear tire model
            Fy = (alpha/pi*180)/5 * Fz * tire_fyFactor
            """
            return (alpha * 180.0 / ca.pi) / 5.0 * Fz * self.mu_y
    
    def split_long_forces(self, F_acc, F_brake):
        """
        Inputs:
          F_acc   >= 0
          F_brake <= 0
        Returns:
          Ff, Fr  (front/rear longitudinal forces)
        """
        Ff_acc = self.power_dist * F_acc
        Fr_acc = (1.0 - self.power_dist) * F_acc

        Ff_brk = self.brake_bias * F_brake
        Fr_brk = (1.0 - self.brake_bias) * F_brake

        Ff = Ff_acc + Ff_brk
        Fr = Fr_acc + Fr_brk

        return Ff, Fr
    
        
    def get_friction_limit(self,v, mass = None, mu_x = None,mu_y = None, cl = None):

        use_mass = mass if mass is not None else self.mass
        use_mu_x = mu_x if mu_x is not None else self.mu_x
        use_mu_y = mu_y if mu_y is not None else self.mu_y
        use_cl = cl if cl is not None else self.cl
        
        F_aero = 0.5 * self.rho * use_cl * v**2

        Fz = use_mass*self.g + F_aero
        #calcualte max force in x
        max_x = use_mu_x*Fz
        #calculate max force in y
        max_y = use_mu_y*Fz
        
        # (max_longitudinal, max_lateral)
        return (max_x, max_y)
    

    def get_fz(self,v,cla_ef):
        #for single track, returns Fz on front and rear
        fz_aero = 0.5*self.rho*cla_ef*v**2
        fz_aero_front = fz_aero*(1-self.cop)
        fz_aero_rear = fz_aero*self.cop

        fz_f = self.fz_static_f + fz_aero_front
        fz_r = self.fz_static_r + fz_aero_rear

        return fz_f,fz_r
    
    def get_tire_dy(self,fx,fz):

        #save option
        #term = (self.mu_y * fz)**2 - fx**2
        #term_safe = ca.log(1 + ca.exp(term))
        #dy =ca.sqrt(term_safe)
        max_lateral_sq = (self.mu_y * fz)**2 - fx**2
        # Použijeme fmax, aby pod odmocninou bylo minimálně 1e-6
        dy = ca.sqrt(ca.fmax(max_lateral_sq, 1))
        return dy

        #dy = ca.sqrt((self.mu_y*fz)**2 -fx**2) 
        #return dy
    
    def get_cornering_stiffness(self, fz):
       
        PKY1 = 19.7276 
        PKY2 = 3.3304
        PKY4 = 7.5668
        FNOMIN = 2000  # Z tir souboru

        # Pacejka vzorec pro Stiffness (bez Camberu)
        # C_alpha = PKY1 * FNOMIN * sin(PKY4 * arctan(Fz / (PKY2 * FNOMIN)))
        
        term_inner = fz / (PKY2 * FNOMIN)
        c_alpha = PKY1 * FNOMIN * ca.sin(PKY4 * ca.arctan(term_inner))
    
        return c_alpha
    
    
    def update_regen_usage(self, base_drift=-0.0015, sigma=0.02):
    
        noise = np.random.normal(0.0, sigma)
        self.regen_eff += np.round(base_drift + noise,2)
        self.regen_eff = round(np.clip(self.regen_eff, 0.5, 1.0),2)

    def update_grip(self, lap):
        
        #tire warmup
        warmup_progress = 1 - np.exp(-(lap + 1) / self.tire_model["warmup_tau"])

        mu_x_cold = self.tire_model["cold_factor"] * self.tire_model["mu_x_nominal"]
        current_base_mu_x = mu_x_cold + (self.tire_model['mu_x_nominal'] - mu_x_cold) * warmup_progress
        
        # Y složka
        mu_y_cold = self.tire_model["cold_factor"] * self.tire_model["mu_y_nominal"]
        current_base_mu_y = mu_y_cold + (self.tire_model['mu_y_nominal'] - mu_y_cold) * warmup_progress

        noise = self.tire_model["noise_std"] * np.random.randn()
      
        self.mu_x = np.round(current_base_mu_x + noise, 3)
        self.mu_y = np.round(current_base_mu_y + noise, 3)

     
        print(f"Lap {lap+1} Grip: Mu_X={self.mu_x}, Mu_Y={self.mu_y} (Warmup: {warmup_progress:.1%})")
        
       # return self.mu_x, self.mu_y

    def singletrack_time_ode(self, z, u, cl_eff=None, cd_eff=None):
            """
            Time-domain bicycle ODE.

            State:
            z = [vx, vy, psi, dpsi, X, Y]

            Input:
            u = [delta_f, F_acc, F_brake]

            Returns:
            dz/dt (CasADi expression)
            """

            vx   = z[0]
            vy   = z[1]
            psi  = z[2]
            r    = z[3]
            X    = z[4]
            Y    = z[5]

            delta_f = u[0]
            F_acc   = u[1]
            F_brake = u[2]

            # effective aero params 
            if cl_eff is None:
                cl_eff = self.cl
            if cd_eff is None:
                cd_eff = self.cd  

            # distribute long forces
            Ff, Fr = self.split_long_forces(F_acc, F_brake)

            # normal loads 
            Fz_f, Fz_r = self.get_fz(vx, cl_eff)

            # slip angles 
            alpha_f = -ca.atan2(
                (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.cos(delta_f) - vx * ca.sin(delta_f),
                (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.sin(delta_f) + vx * ca.cos(delta_f)
            )

            alpha_r = -ca.atan2(
                (vy - self.lr * r), vx
            )

            # lateral tire forces
            F_fy = self.tire_fy(alpha_f, Fz_f)
            F_ry = self.tire_fy(alpha_r, Fz_r)

            # aero drag (uses vx only, cd is CdA)
            F_drag = 0.5 * self.rho * cd_eff * vx**2 + self.fix_drag

            # dynamics (Matlab style)
            dvx = (Ff * ca.cos(delta_f) - F_fy * ca.sin(delta_f) + Fr - F_drag) / self.mass + r * vy
            dvy = (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f) + F_ry) / self.mass - r * vx

            dpsi = r
            dr   = (self.lf * (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f)) - self.lr * F_ry) / self.Iz

            dX = vx * ca.cos(psi) - vy * ca.sin(psi)
            dY = vx * ca.sin(psi) + vy * ca.cos(psi)

            return ca.vertcat(dvx, dvy, dpsi, dr, dX, dY)
    
    def singletrack_path_ode(self, s, z, u, track, cl_eff=None, cd_eff=None):
        """
        Path-domain bicycle ODE.

        Independent variable: s

        State:
          z = [vx, vy, psi, dpsi, n, t]

        Input:
          u = [delta_f, F_acc, F_brake]

        Track must provide interpolation:
          th(s) = track heading
          C(s)  = curvature

        Returns:
          dz/ds
        """

        vx   = z[0]
        vy   = z[1]
        psi  = z[2]
        r    = z[3]
        n    = z[4]
        t    = z[5]

        delta_f = u[0]
        F_acc   = u[1]
        F_brake = u[2]

        # effective aero params
        if cl_eff is None:
            cl_eff = self.cl
        if cd_eff is None:
            cd_eff = self.cd  # CdA

        # distribute long forces
        Ff, Fr = self.split_long_forces(F_acc, F_brake)

        # track geometry 
        th = track.heading(s)     # == track.psi(s)
        C  = track.curvature(s)   # == track.kappa(s)

        eps = psi - th

        
        denom = (vx * ca.cos(eps) - vy * ca.sin(eps))
        Sf = (1.0 - n * C) / denom

        # normal loads depend on vx only
        Fz_f, Fz_r = self.get_fz(vx, cl_eff)

        # slip angles 
        alpha_f = -ca.atan2(
            (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.cos(delta_f) - vx * ca.sin(delta_f),
            (vy + self.lf * r) * ca.sin(delta_f) + vx * ca.cos(delta_f)
        )

        alpha_r = -ca.atan2(
            (vy - self.lr * r), vx
        )

        # lateral tire forces
        F_fy = self.tire_fy(alpha_f, Fz_f)
        F_ry = self.tire_fy(alpha_r, Fz_r)

        # aero drag
        F_drag = 0.5 * self.rho * cd_eff * vx**2 + self.fix_drag

        # time-domain derivatives (same as time ODE)
        dvx_dt = (Ff * ca.cos(delta_f) - F_fy * ca.sin(delta_f) + Fr - F_drag) / self.mass + r * vy
        dvy_dt = (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f) + F_ry) / self.mass - r * vx
        dpsi_dt = r
        dr_dt   = (self.lf * (Ff * ca.sin(delta_f) + F_fy * ca.cos(delta_f)) - self.lr * F_ry) / self.Iz

        # path coordinates dynamics
        dn_dt = vx * ca.sin(eps) + vy * ca.cos(eps)
        dt_dt = 1.0

        # convert dt derivatives to ds derivatives
        dvx_ds  = Sf * dvx_dt
        dvy_ds  = Sf * dvy_dt
        dpsi_ds = Sf * dpsi_dt
        dr_ds   = Sf * dr_dt
        dn_ds   = Sf * dn_dt
        dt_ds   = Sf * dt_dt

        return ca.vertcat(dvx_ds, dvy_ds, dpsi_ds, dr_ds, dn_ds, dt_ds)
