            nom_end = 0.0
        elif total_time < T_min_adj:
            nom_end = 1.0
        else:
            nom_end = (T_max - total_time) / (T_max - T_min_adj)
            
        Endurance_points = (P_max_end - P_min_end) * (nom_end**2) + P_min_end

        if total_time > T_max: 
            Endurance_points = 0.0

        P_max_eff = 75.0
        EF_max = 2 * ef_min
        Ef_team = (total_time**2) * energy_target

        if Ef_team > EF_max:
            nom_eff = 0.0
        elif Ef_team < ef_min:
            nom_eff = 1.0
        else:
            nom_eff = (EF_max - Ef_team) / (EF_max - ef_min)
            
        Efficiency_points = P_max_eff * (nom_eff**2)
        

        return Endurance_points + Efficiency_points


    def solve(self,ef_min=0,T_min=0,objective="points",enforce_full_soc = False,):
            opti = ca.Opti()
            N = self.laps

            E_lap = opti.variable(N)

            
            SoC_start = opti.variable()

            if enforce_full_soc:
                opti.subject_to(SoC_start == 1.0)
        
            
            # Stav baterie (SoC) na začátku každého kola + na úplném konci závodu (N+1)
            SoC = opti.variable(N + 1)    

            MIN_SOC = 0.11

            # ==========================================
            # 3. Počáteční podmínky
            # ==========================================
            opti.subject_to(SoC[0] == SoC_start) 
        

            total_time = 0
            
            for k in range(N):
            
                opti.subject_to(SoC[k+1] == SoC[k] - (E_lap[k] / self.car.acp_capacity))
               
                state_vec = ca.vertcat(SoC[k], E_lap[k], self.car.regen_eff)
                laptime = self.laptime_map(state_vec)
    
                total_time += laptime


    
            opti.subject_to(SoC >= MIN_SOC)
            opti.subject_to(SoC_start >= 0.5)
            opti.subject_to(SoC_start <= 1.0)
            #opti.subject_to(SoC <= 1.0)
            
        
            opti.subject_to(E_lap >= self.e_target_min) 
            opti.subject_to(E_lap <= self.e_target_max) 


            # === INITIAL GUESS ===
            E_guess = 0.25
            opti.set_initial(E_lap, E_guess)
            opti.set_initial(SoC_start, 1.0)
            soc_guess = [1.0 - k * (E_guess / self.car.acp_capacity) for k in range(N + 1)]
            opti.set_initial(SoC, soc_guess)
           
        
            # ==========================================
            # Konstanty pro výpočty 
            # ==========================================
            
            T_max = 1.5 * T_min # Podle tabulky 11 v pravidlech
            
            P_max_end = 250.0
            P_min_end = 0.1 * P_max_end 

            # ==========================================
            # Endurance Score
            # ==========================================
            if objective == "points":
                opti.subject_to(total_time <= T_max) 
            
            nom_end = (T_max - total_time) / (T_max - T_min)
            Endurance_points = (P_max_end - P_min_end) * (nom_end**2) + P_min_end

            # ==========================================
            # Efficiency Score
            # ==========================================
            P_max_eff = 75.0
            EF_max = 2 * ef_min
            Ef_team = (total_time**2) * ca.sum1(E_lap)

            if objective == "points":
                opti.subject_to(Ef_team <= EF_max)

            nom_eff = (EF_max - Ef_team) / (EF_max - ef_min)
            Efficiency_points = P_max_eff * (nom_eff**2)
            
            # ==========================================
            # Objective Function
            # ==========================================
            Total_points = Endurance_points + Efficiency_points
            
            if objective == "points":
                opti.minimize(-Total_points)

            elif objective == "time":
                opti.minimize(total_time)

            elif objective == "efficiency":
                opti.minimize(Ef_team)
            
            # ==========================================
            # 8. Solver settings
            # ==========================================
            p_opts = {"expand": True}
            s_opts = {
                "max_iter": 5000, 
                "tol": 1e-3,                   
                "acceptable_tol": 1e-2,           
                "print_level": 0,
                "hessian_approximation": "limited-memory"
            }
            opti.solver('ipopt', p_opts, s_opts)

            try:
                
                sol = opti.solve()
                
                print(f"Celkový čas: {sol.value(total_time):.2f} s")
                
                # Extrakce optimálních hodnot
                opt_soc = sol.value(SoC)
                opt_e_targets = sol.value(E_lap)
                
                # Ošetření pro případ, že N=1 (CasADi vrací skalár místo pole)
                if N == 1:
                    opt_e_targets = [opt_e_targets]
                    opt_soc = [opt_soc, sol.value(SoC)[1]]

                opt_lap_times = []
                
               
                for k in range(N):
                    # Extrakce konkrétních hodnot pro toto kolo
                    soc_k = float(opt_soc[k])
                    e_k = float(opt_e_targets[k])
                    regen_k = float(self.car.regen_eff)
                    
                    # Výpočet času na kolo z mapy
                    lap_time_k = float(sol.value(self.laptime_map(ca.vertcat(soc_k, e_k, regen_k))))
                    opt_lap_times.append(lap_time_k)
                    
                    
                    if lap_time_k < 20.0: 
                        print(f"   Laptime: {lap_time_k:.2f} s")
                        print(f"   Vstupy -> SoC_start: {soc_k:.4f}, E_lap: {e_k:.4f}, Regen: {regen_k:.2f}")
                        return {"sccess":False}

                opt_total_time = sol.value(total_time)

                return {
                    "success": True,
                    "total_time": opt_total_time,
                    "e_targets": opt_e_targets,     
                    "soc_profile": opt_soc,
                    "soc_start": sol.value(SoC_start),
                    "lap_times": opt_lap_times,
                    "points": sol.value(Total_points),
                    "ef_score": sol.value(Ef_team)
                }
                
            except RuntimeError as e:
                print(f"Solver failed: {e}")
                opti.debug.show_infeasibilities()
                return {"success": False}

       
@staticmethod
def calculate_initial_guess(car,track, init_cond: InitConditions, ds=1.0):
        #creates warm start for the solver based on simple forward-backward integration and curve speed limits
       
        total_length = track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))
        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        
        ds_const = total_length / N 
        
        kappa_dm = track.get_curvature(s_sim)
        kappa = np.array(kappa_dm).flatten()
        
        m = car.mass
        g = 9.81
        rho = 1.225
        Cl = car.cl
        Cd = car.cd
        mu_x = car.mu_x - 0.1
        mu_y = car.mu_y - 0.1
        P_max = car.power_max-5000
        F_max_abs = 8000
        
    
        #curve limits vectorized
        denom = m * np.abs(kappa) - mu_y * 0.5 * rho * Cl
        
      
        v_max_car = 40.0 
        v_curve = np.full(N+1, v_max_car)
        
        
        mask = denom > 0
        v_sq_limit = (mu_y * m * g) / denom[mask]
        v_curve[mask] = np.sqrt(v_sq_limit)
        
    
        v_curve = np.minimum(v_curve, v_max_car)

        #forward integration (acceleration)
        v_fwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_fwd[0] = max(init_cond.v, 3.0) 
        
        for k in range(N):
            v_curr = v_fwd[k]
            
            # Aero Drag
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            
            # Grip: F_z = m*g + AeroDown
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            F_grip = mu_x * F_z
            
            # Power: P = F*v -> F = P/v
            F_power = P_max / max(v_curr, 2.0) * car.power_eff
            
            # Useable accel force
            F_acc_max = min(F_grip, F_power, F_max_abs)
            
            # Net force & accel
            F_net = F_acc_max - F_drag
            acc = F_net / m
            
            # kinematics
            v_next = np.sqrt(max(v_curr**2 + 2 * acc * ds_const, 1.0))
            
            # limit by curve
            v_fwd[k+1] = min(v_next, v_curve[k+1])

        # backward integration (braking)
        v_bwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_bwd[-1] = v_curve[-1] 
        
        for k in range(N, 0, -1):
            v_curr = v_bwd[k] 
            
            # Aero
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            
            # Max Brake Force
            F_grip = mu_x * F_z
            F_brake_max = min(F_grip, F_max_abs) 
                    
            acc_back = (F_brake_max + F_drag) / m
            
            v_prev = np.sqrt(v_curr**2 + 2 * acc_back * ds_const)
            
            # Min (Backward, Forward, Curve)
            v_bwd[k-1] = min(v_prev, v_curve[k-1], v_fwd[k-1])

        # Final velocity profile
        v_guess = v_bwd 
        
        #smoothing for IPOPT
        window_size = 5
        v_smooth = np.convolve(v_guess, np.ones(window_size)/window_size, mode='same')
        v_smooth[0] = v_guess[0] 
        v_smooth[-1] = v_guess[-1] 
        
        #calculate forces and energy for the guess
        v_sq_diff = np.diff(v_smooth**2)
        acc_req = v_sq_diff / (2 * ds_const)
        
        v_mid = 0.5 * (v_smooth[:-1] + v_smooth[1:])
        
        # Drag
        F_drag_vec = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_mid**2
        
        # F
        F_net_req = m * acc_req
        F_long_req = F_net_req + F_drag_vec
        
        # time
        dt_vec = ds_const / np.maximum(v_mid, 1.0)
        T_guess = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(dt_vec))) + init_cond.T
        
        # Inputs splitting
        F_acc_guess = np.maximum(F_long_req, 0)
        F_brake_guess = np.minimum(F_long_req, 0)
        
        # Energy
        P_mech = F_long_req * v_mid
       
        dE = P_mech * dt_vec / 3.6e6 
        E_guess = np.concatenate(([init_cond.E], np.cumsum(dE) + init_cond.E))

        '''
        plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
            
        plt.subplot(4,1,1)
        plt.plot(s_sim, v_smooth * 3.6, label='Speed [km/h]') # s_sim (N+1) vs v (N+1) -> OK
        plt.ylabel('v [km/h]')
        plt.grid(True)
            
        plt.subplot(4,1,2)
            # POZOR: s_sim[:-1] pro síly (protože síly jsou na intervalech)
        plt.plot(s_sim[:-1], F_acc_guess, 'g', label='F Acc [N]') 
        plt.plot(s_sim[:-1], F_brake_guess, 'r', label='F Brake [N]')
        plt.ylabel('Force [N]')
        plt.legend()
        plt.grid(True)
            
        plt.subplot(4,1,3)
        plt.plot(s_sim, E_guess, label='Energy [kWh]') # s_sim (N+1) vs E (N+1) -> OK
        plt.ylabel('E [kWh]')
        plt.grid(True)
            
        plt.subplot(4,1,4)
        plt.plot(s_sim, T_guess, label='Time [s]') # s_sim (N+1) vs T (N+1) -> OK
        plt.ylabel('T [s]')
        plt.xlabel('Distance [m]')
        plt.grid(True)
            
        plt.tight_layout()
        plt.show()
        '''
        return {
            'v': np.array(v_smooth).flatten(),       
            'T': np.array(T_guess).flatten(),        
            'E': np.array(E_guess).flatten(),        
            'F_acc': np.array(F_acc_guess).flatten(),   
            'F_brake': np.array(F_brake_guess).flatten()
        }

@staticmethod
def calculate_initial_guess_single(car, track, init_cond: 'InitConditions', ds=1.0):
        """
    
        1. Longitudinal: Forward-backward integrace
        2. Lateral: Steady-state bicycle model
        """
       
        total_length = track.length
        N = int(np.round(total_length / ds))
        s_sim = np.linspace(0, total_length, N + 1)
        ds_const = total_length / N 
        
        # Track data
        kappa_dm = track.get_curvature(s_sim)
        kappa = np.array(kappa_dm).flatten()
        psi_track = np.array(track.get_heading(s_sim)).flatten()
        
        
        try:
             x_track = np.interp(s_sim, track.s, track.x)
             y_track = np.interp(s_sim, track.s, track.y)
        except:
             x_track = np.zeros_like(s_sim)
             y_track = np.zeros_like(s_sim)

        # Car parametry
        m = car.mass
        g = car.g
        rho = car.rho
     
        Cl = car.cl 
        Cd = car.cd 
        mu_x = car.mu_x * 0.95 # Malá rezerva pro warm start
        mu_y = car.mu_y * 0.95
        
        lf = car.lf
        lr = car.lr
        L = car.wheelbase
        cop = car.cop 

      
        #longitudinal profile
        #   
        v_max_car = 40.0 # [m/s]
        v_curve = np.full(N+1, v_max_car)
        
        # Limit v zatáčkách: m*v^2*k <= mu_y * Fz
        # Fz = mg + 0.5*rho*Cl*v^2
        term_aero = 0.5 * rho * Cl
        denom = m * np.abs(kappa) - mu_y * term_aero
        
        mask = denom > 0
        v_sq_limit = (mu_y * m * g) / denom[mask]
        v_curve[mask] = np.sqrt(v_sq_limit)
        v_curve = np.minimum(v_curve, v_max_car)

        # A) Forward 
        v_fwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_fwd[0] = max(init_cond.v, 5.0)
        
        for k in range(N):
            v_curr = v_fwd[k]
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            
            F_grip_long = mu_x * F_z
            F_power = car.power_max / max(v_curr, 1.0) * car.power_eff
            
            F_acc_avail = min(F_grip_long, F_power) - F_drag
            acc = F_acc_avail / m
            
            v_next = np.sqrt(max(v_curr**2 + 2 * acc * ds_const, 1.0))
            v_fwd[k+1] = min(v_next, v_curve[k+1])

        # B) Backward 
        v_bwd = np.zeros(N+1)
        v_bwd[-1] = v_fwd[-1]
        
        for k in range(N, 0, -1):
            v_curr = v_bwd[k]
            
            # 1. Síly od prostředí
            F_drag = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_curr**2
            F_z = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_curr**2
            
            # 2. Limit přilnavosti (Grip)
            F_grip_long = mu_x * F_z
            
            # 3. Limit výkonu (Power) -> F = P / v
            #    Používáme max(v, 1.0) aby nedošlo k dělení nulou
            F_power_limit = abs(car.brake_max) / max(v_curr, 1.0)
            
            # 4. Určení limitní brzdné síly kol
            #    Auto nemůže brzdit víc, než dovolí grip, A ZÁROVEŇ 
            #    nemůže brzdit víc, než dovolí motory (pokud nemá mechanické brzdy).
            F_brake_effective = min(F_grip_long, F_power_limit)
            
            # 5. Výpočet maximální možné dekelerace
            #    (Brzdná síla kol + Odpor vzduchu) / m
            dec_max = (F_brake_effective + F_drag) / m
            
            # 6. Kinematika zpětného chodu
            #    v_prev^2 = v_curr^2 + 2 * a * s
            v_prev_sq = v_curr**2 + 2 * dec_max * ds_const
            v_prev = np.sqrt(v_prev_sq)
            
            # 7. Aplikace limitů (zatáčka, forward pass)
            v_bwd[k-1] = min(v_prev, v_curve[k-1], v_fwd[k-1])

        v_smooth = v_bwd # Finální rychlost

        #lateral dynamics
    
        F_lat_total = m * v_smooth**2 * kappa
        
        # (Static + Aero distribution)
   
        Fz_total = m*g + 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_smooth**2
        Fz_aero = 0.5 * rho * Cl * v_smooth**2
        
        # Rozdělení Fz
        Fz_f = car.fz_static_f + Fz_aero * (1 - cop)
        Fz_r = car.fz_static_r + Fz_aero * cop
        
        # moment equation to center of mass
        # Fy_f * lf = Fy_r * lr => Fy_r = Fy_f * lf/lr
        # Fy_f + Fy_r = F_lat_total
      
        Fy_f_req = F_lat_total * (lr / L)
        Fy_r_req = F_lat_total * (lf / L)
        
        # --- inverse tire model
        #  Fy = (alpha_deg / 5.0) * Fz * mu_y
        # inverse: alpha_deg = (Fy / (Fz * mu_y)) * 5.0
        
        max_Fy_f = Fz_f * mu_y
        max_Fy_r = Fz_r * mu_y
        
        Fy_f_eff = np.clip(Fy_f_req, -max_Fy_f, max_Fy_f)
        Fy_r_eff = np.clip(Fy_r_req, -max_Fy_r, max_Fy_r)

        OPTIMAL_SLIP_ANGLE_DEG = 6.0

        ratio_f = Fy_f_eff / np.maximum(max_Fy_f, 1.0) # prevence /0
        ratio_r = Fy_r_eff / np.maximum(max_Fy_r, 1.0)
        
        alpha_f_deg = ratio_f * OPTIMAL_SLIP_ANGLE_DEG
        alpha_r_deg = ratio_r * OPTIMAL_SLIP_ANGLE_DEG
        
    
        alpha_f_rad = np.radians(alpha_f_deg)
        alpha_r_rad = np.radians(alpha_r_deg)
        
  
        delta_guess = L * kappa + alpha_f_rad - alpha_r_rad
        
  
        delta_guess = np.clip(delta_guess, -car.steeringAngle_max, car.steeringAngle_max)

        # outputs
        
        acc_req = np.diff(v_smooth**2, prepend=v_smooth[0]**2) / (2 * ds_const)
        F_net = m * acc_req
        F_drag_vec = 0.5 * rho * Cd * v_smooth**2
        F_total_req = F_net + F_drag_vec
        
        F_acc_guess = np.maximum(F_total_req, 0)
        F_brake_guess = np.minimum(F_total_req, 0)
        
   
        dt_vec = ds_const / np.maximum(v_smooth, 1.0)
        t_guess = np.cumsum(dt_vec)
        
        # Yaw rate
        r_guess = v_smooth * kappa

        return {
            'v': v_smooth,
            'vy': np.zeros_like(v_smooth), 
            'r': r_guess,
            'psi': psi_track,
            'x': x_track,
            'y': y_track,
            'n': np.zeros_like(v_smooth),
            'xi': np.zeros_like(v_smooth), # Heading error relative to track
            'F_acc': F_acc_guess[:-1],
            'F_brake': F_brake_guess[:-1],
            'delta': delta_guess[:-1],
            't': t_guess,
            's': s_sim,
            'drs_usage':np.zeros(N)
        }
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\solver.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\track_generation_thread.py ---
from PyQt6.QtCore import QThread, pyqtSignal
from src.core.trackGenerator import TrackGenerator


class TrackGenerationWorker(QThread):
    finished = pyqtSignal(bool, str) # success, message/path

    def __init__(self, data, lap_info, is_closed, save_path):
        super().__init__()
        self.data = data
        self.lap_info = lap_info
        self.is_closed = is_closed
        self.save_path = save_path

    def run(self):
        try:
        
            success,msg = TrackGenerator.process_and_save_lap(
                self.data, 
                self.lap_info, 
                self.is_closed, 
                self.save_path
            )
            
            if success:
                self.finished.emit(True, self.save_path)
            else:
                self.finished.emit(False, "Saving track failed: " + msg)
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.finished.emit(False, str(e))
--- KONEC SOUBORU: src\optimization\track_generation_thread.py ---

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\optimization\work_thread.py ---
from PyQt6.QtCore import QThread, pyqtSignal
from src.optimization.solver import InitConditions, SolverResults, calculate_initial_guess, calculate_initial_guess_single
from src.core.track import Track
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import casadi as ca
from scipy.interpolate import RegularGridInterpolator, griddata
import time


@dataclass
class SolverPayload:
    kind: str  #type of solver run
    data: object #data
    meta: dict = field(default_factory=dict) #additional meta info


MODE_SINGLE = 0
MODE_ENDURANCE = 1
MODE_ACP_TEST = 2
MODE_STRATEGY_OPT = 3
MODE_DEPLOYMENT_OPT = 4


class SolverWorker(QThread):
    # signals for communication with the main window
    finished = pyqtSignal(object) # returns res
    error = pyqtSignal(str)
    progress = pyqtSignal(int, int)     

    def __init__(self, solver, car, track,ds,mode,laps=1,laptime_map = None):
        super().__init__()
        self.solver = solver
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.laps = laps
        self.ds = ds
        self.mode = mode
        self.laptime_map = laptime_map
    
    def run(self):
        try:
            if self.mode == MODE_SINGLE:
                self.run_single_lap()
            elif self.mode == MODE_ENDURANCE:
                self.run_endurance()
            elif self.mode == MODE_ACP_TEST:
                self.run_acp_test()
            elif self.mode == MODE_STRATEGY_OPT:
                self.run_endurance_opt()
            elif self.mode == MODE_DEPLOYMENT_OPT:  
                self.run_deployment_opt()
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.error.emit(str(e))

    def run_single_lap(self):
        
        start = time.time()
        #start from the same speed as in the real log
        if self.track.speed is not None:
            init_cond = InitConditions(v=self.track.speed[0], T=0.0, E=2.0)
        else:
            init_cond = InitConditions(v=5, T=0.0, E=0.0)


        #calculate initial guess for the solver to speed up convergence
        init_guess = calculate_initial_guess_single(self.car,self.track,init_cond, ds=self.ds)
        '''
        res = SolverResults(
            success=True,
            lap_time=init_guess['t'][-1],
            t=init_guess['t'],
            v=init_guess['v'],
            s=init_guess['s'],
            n=init_guess['n'],
            E=np.zeros_like(init_guess['t']),
            f_acc=init_guess['F_acc'],
            f_brake= init_guess['F_brake'],
            delta=init_guess['delta'],
            drs_usage=init_guess['drs_usage']
        )
        '''
        
        # solver
        if self.solver.rk4 == False:
            res = self.solver.solveLap(init_cond, ds=self.ds,init_guess=init_guess)
        else:
            res = self.solver.solveLap_rk4(init_cond, ds=self.ds,init_guess=init_guess)
        
        end = time.time()

        print(f"Time elapsed: {end - start:.6f} s")
        
        if not res.success:
            raise Exception("Optimization failed for single lap.")
        
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="single_lap",
            data=res,
            meta={"ds": self.ds}
        )
            
        self.finished.emit(payload)

    def run_endurance_opt(self):
        ef_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="efficiency")
        if ef_min_res["success"]:
            ef_min = ef_min_res["ef_score"]
            print(f"opt ef score {ef_min}")
        else:
            ef_min = self.solver.ef_guess(4)
            print(f"fallback ef score {ef_min}")

        #fix time delta
        t_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="time")
        if t_min_res["success"]:
            t_min = t_min_res["total_time"]
            print(f"opt min time {t_min}")
        else:
            t_min = self.solver.best_T_guess(0)
            print(f"fallback min time {t_min}")
      

        regen_targets = np.linspace(0.15, 1.0, num=10, endpoint=True)
        delta_targets = np.linspace(0.0, 0.15, num=10, endpoint=True)

        # POZOR NA ZÁVORKY: np.zeros((x, y)) musí mít tuple!
        e_opt_grid = np.zeros((len(regen_targets), len(delta_targets)))
        soc_opt_grid = np.zeros((len(regen_targets), len(delta_targets)))

        current_step =0
        total_steps = len(regen_targets)*len(delta_targets)

        for i, regen_target in enumerate(regen_targets):
            for j, delta_target in enumerate(delta_targets):

                current_step += 1
                self.progress.emit(current_step, total_steps)
                
                self.car.regen_eff = regen_target
                t_min_adj = t_min*(1-delta_target)
              
                # Vyřešíme optimum přes CasADi
                res = self.solver.solve(ef_min, t_min_adj, objective="points")
                
                if res and res.get("success", False):
                    # res["e_targets"] je pole spotřeby na kolo, sečteme ho pro celkovou energii
                    e_opt_grid[i, j] = np.sum(res["e_targets"])
                    soc_opt_grid[i, j] = res["soc_start"]
                else:
                    # Pokud solver pro danou kombinaci nenajde řešení, dáme tam NaN
                    e_opt_grid[i, j] = np.nan
                    soc_opt_grid[i, j] = np.nan

        # Vytvoření DVOU 2D interpolantů (method='linear' je lepší, pokud máme v gridu nějaké NaN hodnoty ze selhání)
        e_interpolant = RegularGridInterpolator(
            (regen_targets, delta_targets), e_opt_grid, method='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan
        )
        soc_interpolant = RegularGridInterpolator(
            (regen_targets, delta_targets), soc_opt_grid, method='linear', bounds_error=False, fill_value=np.nan
        )
     
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="endurance_map",
            data=None,
            meta={
                "ef_min": ef_min,
                "t_min": t_min,
                "e_interpolant": e_interpolant,      #
                "soc_interpolant": soc_interpolant,  
                "grid_regen": regen_targets,
                "grid_delta": delta_targets,
                "z_min_e": np.nanmin(e_opt_grid),    
                "z_max_e": np.nanmax(e_opt_grid)
            }
        )

        self.finished.emit(payload)

    def run_deployment_opt(self):
        """
        Vypočítá pouze jedno konkrétní nasazení strategie (jeden bod)
        pro zadaný Regen a Delta a vrátí detailní data pro StrategyResultTab.
        """
        # 1. Získání baseline hodnot (stejně jako u gridu)
        ef_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="efficiency")
        if ef_min_res["success"]:
            ef_min = ef_min_res["ef_score"]
            print(f"opt ef score {ef_min}")
        else:
            ef_min = self.solver.ef_guess(4)
            print(f"fallback ef score {ef_min}")


        #fix time delta
        t_min_res = self.solver.solve(0,0,objective="time")
        if t_min_res["success"]:
            t_min = t_min_res["total_time"]
            print(f"opt min time {t_min}")
        else:
            t_min = self.solver.best_T_guess(0)
            print(f"fallback min time {t_min}")
      

        # 2. Úprava času soupeře podle zadané delty
        t_min_adj = t_min * (1 - self.car.performance_delta)

        # 3. Spuštění optimalizace pro tyto konkrétní podmínky
        res_opt = self.solver.solve(ef_min, t_min_adj,objective="points")

        res_full = self.solver.solve(ef_min,t_min_adj,objective="points",enforce_full_soc=True)

        # 4. Vytvoření payloadu
        # Použijeme kind="endurance_deployment", na který vaše UI už čeká
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="endurance_deployment",
            data={
                "opt_soc": res_opt,
                "full_soc": res_full
            },
            meta={
                "ef_min": ef_min,
                "t_min": t_min,
                "regen_eff": self.car.regen_eff,
                "delta": self.car.performance_delta
            }
        )

        # 5. Odeslání výsledků do hlavního vlákna
        self.finished.emit(payload)


    def run_acp_test(self):
        
        start = time.time()

        total_race_distance = 22000.0 # [m]
        laps = int(np.round(total_race_distance / self.track.length))

        #battery_scaling_factor = 0.22 # kWh/kg
        original_mass = self.car.mass
        original_regen = self.car.regen_eff

        # grid setup
        acp_targets = np.linspace(3.5,10.0,num=15,endpoint=True)
        e_targets = np.round(acp_targets/self.laps,2)
        
        regen_targets = np.linspace(0.0, 1.1, num=15, endpoint=True)

        soc_targets = np.linspace(0.08,1.05,num=15,endpoint=True)

        total_steps = len(e_targets) * len(regen_targets)*len(soc_targets)
        results_log = []
        
        current_step = 0

        flat_lap_times = []

        last_slowest_lap = 40

        prev_res = None

        for soc in soc_targets:
            for e_target in e_targets:
                for regen in regen_targets:

                    current_step += 1
                    self.progress.emit(current_step, total_steps)
                    
                    # 1. Kontrola, zda je to vůbec fyzikálně možné
                    if soc * self.car.acp_capacity < e_target:
                        flat_lap_times.append(np.nan)
                        continue
                    
                    # 2. Nastavení auta a počátečních podmínek
                    self.car.regen_eff = regen
                    self.car.energy_limit = e_target
                    E_start = np.round(self.car.acp_capacity*(1-soc),2)
                    init_cond = InitConditions(v=self.track.speed[0]-2,T=0.0,E=E_start)
                    print(f"E_start :{E_start}, SoC: {soc} E target: {e_target} Regen: {regen}")
                    res = self.solver.solveLap_rk4(
                        init_cond=init_cond,
                        ds=self.ds,
                        init_guess=prev_res
                    )

                    
                    if res.success:
                        laptime = res.lap_time
                        flat_lap_times.append(laptime)
                        prev_res = res
                        if laptime > last_slowest_lap:
                            last_slowest_lap = laptime

                        # Uložíme i data pro PyQt UI (raw body)
                        res.meta_soc = soc
                        res.meta_energy = e_target
                        res.meta_regen = regen
                        res.meta_mass = self.car.mass
                        results_log.append(res)
                    else:
                        flat_lap_times.append(np.nan) 


        # --- Záchrana B-spline gridu: Vyplnění NaN hodnot ---
        flat_lap_times = np.array(flat_lap_times, dtype=float)
        
        if np.isnan(flat_lap_times).any():
            print("Nalezeny neplatné body (fyzika/solver). Opravuji pro B-spline...")
            
            # Vytvoření souřadnicové sítě přesně odpovídající tvým vnořeným for cyklům
            S, E_grid, R = np.meshgrid(soc_targets, e_targets, regen_targets, indexing='ij')
            
            # Spojení do matice bodů [N, 3]
            points = np.column_stack((S.ravel(), E_grid.ravel(), R.ravel()))
            values = flat_lap_times
            
            # Maska platných hodnot
            valid_mask = ~np.isnan(values)
            
            if valid_mask.any():
                valid_points = points[valid_mask]
                valid_values = values[valid_mask]
                
                # Vyplnění NaN bodů nejbližší platnou hodnotou
                filled_values = griddata(valid_points, valid_values, points, method='nearest')
                
                # Přepíšeme pole s lap časy vyčištěnými daty
                flat_lap_times = filled_values
            else:
                print("KRITICKÁ CHYBA: Solver selhal na všech bodech mřížky!")

        end = time.time()

        print(f"Time elapsed: {end - start:.6f} s")

   
        np.savez_compressed(
            f"laptime_map{self.track.name}_15_15_15.npz",
            soc_targets=np.array(soc_targets),
            e_targets=np.array(e_targets),
            regen_targets=np.array(regen_targets),
            flat_lap_times=np.array(flat_lap_times), 
            laps=np.array(self.laps),
            acp_capacity=np.array(self.car.acp_capacity)
        )

    
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="acp_test",
            data=results_log, # Raw data pro scatter ploty v UI
            meta={
                "laps": laps
            }
        )

        self.finished.emit(payload)
        '''
        for r_idx, regen_val in enumerate(regen_targets):
           
            self.car.regen_eff = regen_val

            prev_res = None 

            for e_idx, e_target in enumerate(e_targets):
                current_step += 1
                self.progress.emit(current_step, total_steps)

                #recalculate battery mass based on energy target
                #capacity_needed = laps * e_target
                #delta_cap = capacity_needed - self.car.acp_capacity # kWh
                #weight_delta = delta_cap / battery_scaling_factor
                
               # self.car.mass = original_mass + weight_delta
                
                # Init conditions for the lap
                current_cond = InitConditions(v=10.0, T=0.0, E=0.0)
                self.car.energy_limit = e_target 
                
                # run the solver
                try:
                    res = self.solver.solveLap(
                        init_cond=current_cond,
                        ds=self.ds,
                        init_guess=prev_res # warm start
                    )
                    
                    if res.success:

                        res.meta_energy = e_target
                        res.meta_regen = regen_val
                        res.meta_mass = self.car.mass
                        
                        results_log.append(res)
                        prev_res = res # use as warm start for next
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Solver fail at Regen={regen_val}, E={e_target}: {e}")

        #return car to original state
        self.car.mass = original_mass
        self.car.regen_eff = original_regen

        if not results_log:
            raise Exception("Sensitivity test failed: No feasible solution found.")
        
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="acp_test",
            data=results_log,
            meta={"ahoj":1}
        )

        self.finished.emit(payload)
    '''
    def run_drs_test(self):

        #calculate energy targets
        acp_targets = np.linspace(4.2,6.5, num=5, endpoint=True) #acp capacity from 4.2 kwH to 6.5 kwH
        energy_targets = acp_targets/self.laps
        results_drs = []
        results_no_drs = []

        current_step = 0

        print(f"laps: {self.laps}")

        total_steps = len(energy_targets) * 2  # DRS and no-DRS for each target
        for target in energy_targets:
            current_step += 1
            self.progress.emit(current_step, total_steps)

            current_cond = InitConditions(v=self.track.speed[1], T=0.0, E=0.0)
            # run the solver with DRS enabled
            self.car.drs_allowed = True
            self.car.energy_limit = target
            try:
                res_drs = self.solver.solveLap(
                    init_cond=current_cond,
                    ds=self.ds
                )

                laptime = res_drs.lap_time
                energy_used = res_drs.E[-1]
                drs_usage = res_drs.drs_usage
                drs_active_ratio = (np.sum(drs_usage > 0.9) / drs_usage.size) * 100.0
                ef_score = energy_used*self.laps*(laptime*self.laps)**2

                results_drs.append({'target': target*self.laps, 'laptime': laptime, 'energy_used': energy_used, 'drs_active_ratio': drs_active_ratio, 'ef_score': ef_score})
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"DRS test failed for target {target}: {e}")

            # run the solver without DRS
            self.car.drs_allowed = False
            current_step += 1
            self.progress.emit(current_step, total_steps)
            try:
                res_no_drs = self.solver.solveLap(
                    init_cond=current_cond,
                    ds=self.ds
                )
                laptime = res_no_drs.lap_time
                energy_used = res_no_drs.E[-1]
                ef_score = energy_used*self.laps*(laptime*self.laps)**2
                results_no_drs.append({'target': target*self.laps, 'laptime': laptime, 'energy_used': energy_used, 'ef_score': ef_score})

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"No-DRS test failed for target {target}: {e}")
            

        #export results
        payload = SolverPayload(
        kind="drs_test",
        data={
            "drs": results_drs,
            "nodrs": results_no_drs
        },
        meta={"ds": self.ds})

        self.finished.emit(payload)

    def run_endurance(self):
        # we start from 5 ms
        current_cond = InitConditions(v=5.0, T=0.0, E=0.0)
        endurance_log = []
        prev_res = None
        #set the tires to be cold for first lap
        self.car.mu_x = self.car.tire_model['cold_factor']*self.car.tire_model['mu_x_nominal']
        self.car.mu_y = self.car.tire_model['cold_factor']*self.car.tire_model['mu_y_nominal']
        print(f"Starting endurance simulation ({self.solver.laps} laps)...")
        self.car.energy_limit = self.car.acp_capacity/self.laps
        for i in range(self.laps):
            # send info progress
            self.progress.emit(i + 1, self.laps)
            print(f"Lap {i+1} out of {self.laps}")

            #solve current
            res = self.solver.solveLap(
                    init_cond=current_cond, 
                    ds=self.ds,
                    init_guess=prev_res)
                
            if not res.success:
                raise Exception(f"Endurance failed at lap {i+1}")
            
            #save the results
            res.lap_id = i + 1
            res.mu_x = self.car.mu_x       
            res.mu_y = self.car.mu_y
            res.regen_eff = self.car.regen_eff 
            endurance_log.append(res)

            #get the last used mu
            last_mu = self.car.mu_y
            #update the driver error
            self.car.update_grip(i)
            ratio = self.car.mu_y / last_mu
            safe_v = res.v[-1] * np.sqrt(ratio)
            #next lap starting conditions are the conditions we finished this lap
            current_cond.v = safe_v #this is scaled for the new mu change, so it doesnt crash
            current_cond.T = res.t[-1]
            current_cond.E = res.E[-1]
            E_remaining = self.car.acp_capacity - current_cond.E
            #target for next lap:
            laps_remaining = self.laps-(i+1)

            if laps_remaining > 0:
                self.car.energy_limit = E_remaining/laps_remaining
            else:
                self.car.energy_limit = E_remaining
           
            #update the regen factor
            self.car.update_regen_usage()
            print(f"Car regen factor: {self.car.regen_eff}")

            
        payload = SolverPayload(
            kind="endurance",
            data=endurance_log,
            meta={"ds": self.ds, "laps": self.laps, "final_energy": current_cond.E}
        )
        self.finished.emit(payload)

import numpy as np
import pyqtgraph as pg
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QTabWidget)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qt5agg import FigureCanvasQTAgg
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
from matplotlib.colors import TwoSlopeNorm
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QComboBox, QHBoxLayout
import seaborn as sns


class MplCanvas(FigureCanvasQTAgg):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, width=5, height=4, dpi=100):
        
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(width, height), dpi=dpi)
        self.axes = self.fig.add_subplot(111)
        
        super(MplCanvas, self).__init__(self.fig)
        self.setParent(parent)
        
       
        FigureCanvasQTAgg.updateGeometry(self)


class ComparisonPlotWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, selected_data):
        super().__init__()
        self.selected_data = selected_data
        self.runs = []
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Log comparison")
        self.resize(1000, 800)
        
        # 1. Načtení dat
        self.load_data()
        
        # 2. Layout
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # Tabs
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        self.init_dynamics_tab()
        self.init_energy_tab()
        self.init_map_tab()
        
    def load_data(self):
        self.runs = []
        for item in self.selected_data:
            try:
                data = np.load(item['path'], allow_pickle=True)
                run_dict = {
                    's': data['s'],
                    'v': data['v'],
                    't': data['t'],
                    'E': data['E'],
                    'x': data['x'], 
                    'y': data['y'], 
                    'f_acc': data['f_acc'],
                    'f_brake': data['f_brake'],
                    'time': float(data['lap_time']),
                    'name': item['legend']
                }
                self.runs.append(run_dict)
                data.close()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")

        if not self.runs: return

        # Sort: nejrychlejší první
        self.runs.sort(key=lambda x: x['time'])
        self.ref_run = self.runs[0]

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # Grafy
        plot_v = pg.PlotWidget(title="Speed (km/h)")
        plot_v.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        plot_v.setLabel('left', 'Speed', units='km/h')
        plot_v.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        
        plot_dt = pg.PlotWidget(title="Delta Time (s)")
        plot_dt.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        plot_dt.setXLink(plot_v) # Jediné propojení X osy
        plot_dt.setLabel('left', 'Delta Time', units='s')
        plot_dt.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        

        # Vykreslení
        for i, run in enumerate(self.runs):
            # Barva: Reference zelená, ostatní červená/modrá/žlutá
            color = 'b' if run is self.ref_run else (i, len(self.runs)) 
            width = 3 if run is self.ref_run else 2
            
            # Speed
            plot_v.plot(run['s'], run['v'] * 3.6, pen=pg.mkPen(color, width=width))
            
            # Delta Time
            # Musíme ošetřit délky polí, aby šlo odečítat
            min_len = min(len(run['t']), len(self.ref_run['t']))
            
            t_run = run['t'][:min_len]
            t_ref = self.ref_run['t'][:min_len]
            s_axis = run['s'][:min_len]
            
            delta = t_run - t_ref
            plot_dt.plot(s_axis, delta, pen=pg.mkPen(color, width=width))

        layout.addWidget(plot_v)
        layout.addWidget(plot_dt)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics")

    def init_energy_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        plot_p = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power (kW)")
        plot_p.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        plot_p.setLabel('left', 'Power', units='kW')
        plot_p.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        
        plot_e = pg.PlotWidget(title="Energy (kWh)")
        plot_e.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        plot_e.setXLink(plot_p)
        plot_e.setLabel('left', 'Energy', units='kWh')
        plot_e.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')

        for i, run in enumerate(self.runs):
            color = 'b' if run is self.ref_run else (i, len(self.runs))
            width = 3 if run is self.ref_run else 2
            
            # Power Calc
            min_len = min(len(run['f_acc']), len(run['v']))
            f_tot = run['f_acc'][:min_len] + run['f_brake'][:min_len]
            v = run['v'][:min_len]
            pwr = (f_tot * v) / 1000.0
            s_axis = run['s'][:min_len]
            
            plot_p.plot(s_axis, pwr, pen=pg.mkPen(color, width=width))
            plot_e.plot(run['s'], run['E'], pen=pg.mkPen(color, width=width))

        layout.addWidget(plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Energy")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        """
        Vytvoří záložku s Matplotlib mapou pro porovnání Delta V
        """
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # --- Controls (Výběr kola pro porovnání) ---
        controls_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        controls_layout.addWidget(QLabel(f"Reference Lap: {self.ref_run['name']}"))
        controls_layout.addWidget(QLabel("  vs  "))
        
        self.combo_compare = QComboBox()
        # Přidáme do výběru všechna kola kromě referenčního
        self.compare_candidates = [r for r in self.runs if r is not self.ref_run]
        
        if not self.compare_candidates:
            layout.addWidget(QLabel("Need at least 2 logs to show comparison map."))
            widget.setLayout(layout)
            self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Delta V Map")
            return

        for run in self.compare_candidates:
            self.combo_compare.addItem(f"{run['name']} (+{run['time'] - self.ref_run['time']:.2f}s)")
            
        self.combo_compare.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.update_heatmap)
        controls_layout.addWidget(self.combo_compare)
        controls_layout.addStretch()
        
        layout.addLayout(controls_layout)

        # --- Matplotlib Canvas ---
        self.map_canvas = MplCanvas(self, width=5, height=4, dpi=100)
        layout.addWidget(self.map_canvas)
        
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Delta V Map")
        
        # První vykreslení
        self.update_heatmap()

    def update_heatmap(self):
        """
        Vypočítá rozdíl rychlostí a vykreslí mapu.
        """
        if not self.compare_candidates: return

        idx = self.combo_compare.currentIndex()
        comp_run = self.compare_candidates[idx]
        ref_run = self.ref_run

        # 1. Interpolace dat (aby seděla na stejné 's')
        # Použijeme 's' z referenčního kola jako osu
        s_ref = ref_run['s']
        v_ref = ref_run['v'] * 3.6 # km/h
        
        # Interpolujeme rychlost porovnávaného kola na pozice reference
        v_comp_interp = np.interp(s_ref, comp_run['s'], comp_run['v']) * 3.6
        
        # 2. Delta V (Kladná = comp je rychlejší, Záporná = comp je pomalejší)
        delta_v = v_comp_interp - v_ref

        # 3. Vykreslení
        self.map_canvas.axes.clear()
        ax = self.map_canvas.axes
        
        # Vykreslíme trať pomocí Scatter plotu, kde barva = delta_v
        # Používáme colormap 'seismic' (modrá-bílá-červená) nebo 'coolwarm'
        # Normování: 0 musí být bílá/střed. 
        
        # Zjištění limitů pro symetrickou barevnou škálu
        max_diff = max(abs(np.min(delta_v)), abs(np.max(delta_v)))
        if max_diff == 0: max_diff = 1 # Ochrana proti dělení nulou
        
        norm = TwoSlopeNorm(vmin=-max_diff, vcenter=0, vmax=max_diff) 
        #cmap = sns.color_palette("pla", as_cmap=True)
        
        sc = ax.scatter(ref_run['x'], ref_run['y'], c=delta_v, cmap='plasma', s=10, norm=norm)
        
        ax.set_title(f"Speed delta")
        ax.set_aspect('equal')
        ax.set_xlabel("X [m]")
        ax.set_ylabel("Y [m]")
        ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
        
        # Colorbar (pokud už existuje, musíme ho odstranit, jinak se budou množit)
        if hasattr(self, 'cbar'):
            self.cbar.remove()
        
        self.cbar = self.map_canvas.fig.colorbar(sc, ax=ax, label='Delta Speed [km/h]')
        
        # Červená = Pomalejší než ref, Modrá = Rychlejší než ref
        self.map_canvas.draw()

--- ZAČÁTEK SOUBORU: src\ui\drs_plot.py ---
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout
import pyqtgraph as pg
import numpy as np


class DRSResultsWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, drs_data, nodrs_data):
        super().__init__()

        self.drs_data = drs_data
        self.nodrs_data = nodrs_data

        self.setWindowTitle("DRS analysis")
        self.resize(1100, 800)

        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        self.win = pg.GraphicsLayoutWidget()
        layout.addWidget(self.win)

        self.plot_results()

    def plot_results(self):
        # Extract arrays
        E_drs = np.array([d["target"] for d in self.drs_data])
        T_drs = np.array([d["laptime"] for d in self.drs_data])
        U_drs = np.array([d["drs_active_ratio"] for d in self.drs_data])
        Ef_drs = np.array([d["ef_score"] for d in self.drs_data])

        E_no = np.array([d["target"] for d in self.nodrs_data])
        T_no = np.array([d["laptime"] for d in self.nodrs_data])
        Ef_no = np.array([d["ef_score"] for d in self.nodrs_data])

        # Sort by energy
        idx_drs = np.argsort(E_drs)
        idx_no = np.argsort(E_no)

        E_drs, T_drs, U_drs, Ef_drs = E_drs[idx_drs], T_drs[idx_drs], U_drs[idx_drs], Ef_drs[idx_drs]
        E_no, T_no, Ef_no = E_no[idx_no], T_no[idx_no], Ef_no[idx_no]

        # Delta time (positive = DRS helped)
        T_delta = T_no - T_drs

        # --- Plot 1: Delta Time ---
        p1 = self.win.addPlot(title="DRS time gain per lap vs energy target")
        p1.setLabel("bottom", "Endurance energy target", units="kWh")
        p1.setLabel("left", "Delta time", units="s")
        p1.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        p1.invertX(True)

        p1.plot(
            E_drs, T_delta,
            pen=pg.mkPen("b", width=3),
            symbol="o",
            symbolSize=8
        )

        #efficiency score plot
        self.win.nextRow()
        p2 = self.win.addPlot(title="Efficiency score (lower better)")
        p2.setLabel("bottom", "Endurance energy target", units="kWh/lap")
        p2.setLabel("left", "Efficiency score", units="kWh·s²")
        p2.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        p2.setXLink(p1)
        p2.invertX(True)
        p2.addLegend(offset=(30, 30))
      
        p2.plot(
            E_drs, Ef_drs,
            pen=pg.mkPen("r", width=3),
            symbol="o",
            symbolSize=8,
            name="With DRS"
        )
        p2.plot(
            E_no, Ef_no,
            pen=pg.mkPen("orange", width=3),
            symbol="s",
            symbolSize=8,
            name="No DRS"
        )

        self.win.nextRow()

        p3 = self.win.addPlot(title="DRS usage")
        p3.setLabel("bottom", "Endurance energy target", units="kWh")
        p3.setLabel("left", "DRS active ratio", units="%")
        p3.showGrid(x=True, y=True, alpha=0.1)
        p3.setXLink(p1)
        p3.invertX(True)
   

        p3.plot(
            E_drs, U_drs,
            pen=pg.mkPen("g", width=3),
            symbol="o",
            symbolSize=8
        )

import datetime
import os
import numpy as np
import pyqtgraph as pg
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel, 
                             QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QGridLayout, QFileDialog, 
                             QComboBox, QApplication, QPushButton)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from src.utils.saver import save_results_to_npz

class EnduranceResultsWindow(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, results_list, car, track, ds):
        super().__init__()
        self.results_list = results_list
        self.car = car
        self.track = track
        self.ds = ds
        
        # default to first lap
        self.current_idx = 0
        self.current_res = self.results_list[0]

        self.resize(1100, 800)
        self.center_window()
        self.setWindowTitle("Endurance simulation results")
        
        # layout setup
        self.layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.init_header()
        self.init_summary_panel()
        
        # tabs setup
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        self.layout.addWidget(self.tabs)
        
        self.init_dynamics_tab()
        self.init_powertrain_tab()
        self.init_forces_tab()
        self.init_map_tab()
        self.init_evolution_tab()
        
        # initial render
        self.update_view()

    def center_window(self):
        qr = self.frameGeometry()
        cp = QApplication.primaryScreen().availableGeometry().center()
        qr.moveCenter(cp)
        self.move(qr.topLeft())

    def init_header(self):
        """top bar with lap selection and save button."""
        header_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        header_layout.addWidget(QLabel("<b>Select Lap:</b>"))
        
        # lap selection combo
        self.combo_laps = QComboBox()
        self.combo_laps.setFixedWidth(150)
        items = [f"Lap {i+1}" for i in range(len(self.results_list))]
        self.combo_laps.addItems(items)
        self.combo_laps.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_lap_changed)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.combo_laps)
        
        header_layout.addStretch()
        
        # save button
        self.btn_save = QPushButton("Save All (.npz)")
        self.btn_save.setFixedWidth(150)
        self.btn_save.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_save.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #17a2b8; color: white; font-weight: bold; 
                padding: 8px; border-radius: 4px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #138496; }
        """)
        self.btn_save.clicked.connect(self.on_save_clicked)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.btn_save)
        
        self.layout.addLayout(header_layout)

    def on_lap_changed(self, index):
        self.current_idx = index
        self.current_res = self.results_list[index]
        self.update_view()

    def init_summary_panel(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Lap Summary")
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        stats_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(stats_widget)
        grid.setContentsMargins(10, 10, 10, 10)
        grid.setHorizontalSpacing(30)
        grid.setVerticalSpacing(10)
        
        # labels
        self.lbl_lap_time = QLabel("0.000")
        self.lbl_energy = QLabel("0.000")
        self.lbl_max_v = QLabel("0.0")
        self.lbl_avg_v = QLabel("0.0")
        self.lbl_soc = QLabel("0.0")

        style = "color: #007bff; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold; font-family: Consolas;"
        for lbl in [self.lbl_lap_time, self.lbl_energy, self.lbl_max_v, self.lbl_avg_v, self.lbl_soc]:
            lbl.setStyleSheet(style)
            lbl.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignLeft)

        def add_stat_to_grid(row, col_offset, label_text, value_widget, unit_text):
            lbl_name = QLabel(f"{label_text}:")
            lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; font-size: 14px;")
            lbl_name.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter)
            
            lbl_unit = QLabel(unit_text)
            lbl_unit.setStyleSheet("color: #888;")
            
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0 + col_offset)
            grid.addWidget(value_widget, row, 1 + col_offset)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_unit, row, 2 + col_offset)

        # left column
        add_stat_to_grid(0, 0, "Lap Time", self.lbl_lap_time, "s")
        add_stat_to_grid(1, 0, "Max Speed", self.lbl_max_v, "km/h")
        add_stat_to_grid(2, 0, "Cumulative Energy", self.lbl_soc, "kWh")

        # right column
        add_stat_to_grid(0, 4, "Energy Used (Lap)", self.lbl_energy, "kWh")
        add_stat_to_grid(1, 4, "Avg Speed", self.lbl_avg_v, "km/h")
        
        grid.setColumnMinimumWidth(3, 40) 

        layout.addWidget(stats_widget)
        group.setLayout(layout)
        self.layout.addWidget(group)

    def init_dynamics_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # speed plot
        self.plot_v = pg.PlotWidget(title="Speed profile")
        self.plot_v.setLabel('left', 'Speed', units='km/h')
        self.plot_v.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.curve_v = self.plot_v.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2))
        
        # time plot
        self.plot_t = pg.PlotWidget(title="Time Accumulation (Relative to Lap Start)")
        self.plot_t.setLabel('left', 'Time', units='s')
        self.plot_t.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_t.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_t.setXLink(self.plot_v)
        self.curve_t = self.plot_t.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('r', width=2))
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_v)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_t)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Dynamics")

    def init_powertrain_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # power plot
        self.plot_p = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power Usage")
        self.plot_p.setLabel('left', 'Power', units='kW')
        self.plot_p.showGrid(x=True, y=False)
        self.curve_acc = self.plot_p.plot([], [], pen='g', name="Acceleration", width=3)
        self.curve_reg = self.plot_p.plot([], [], pen='r', name="Regen", width=3)
        
        # energy plot
        self.plot_e = pg.PlotWidget(title="Energy Consumption (Cumulative)")
        self.plot_e.setLabel('left', 'Energy', units='kWh')
        self.plot_e.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_e.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_e.setXLink(self.plot_p)
        self.curve_e = self.plot_e.plot([], [], pen=pg.mkPen('orange', width=2))
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_p)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_e)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Powertrain")

    def init_forces_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        self.plot_f = pg.PlotWidget(title="Longitudinal Forces")
        self.plot_f.setLabel('left', 'Force', units='N')
        self.plot_f.setLabel('bottom', 'Distance', units='m')
        self.plot_f.showGrid(x=True, y=True)
        self.plot_f.addLegend()
        
        self.curve_f_acc = self.plot_f.plot([], [], pen='g', name="Traction")
        self.curve_f_brake = self.plot_f.plot([], [], pen='r', name="Braking")
        
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_f)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Forces")

    def init_map_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        self.map_plot = pg.PlotWidget(title="Power Map")
        self.map_plot.setAspectLocked(True)
        self.map_plot.setLabel('bottom', 'X Position', units='m')
        self.map_plot.setLabel('left', 'Y Position', units='m')

        # scatter item
        self.scatter = pg.ScatterPlotItem(size=6, pen=None, hoverable=True, hoverSize=10)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.scatter)
        
        # markers
        self.start_marker = pg.ScatterPlotItem(size=15, symbol='o', brush='w')
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.start_marker)
        
        self.cursor_label = pg.TextItem(anchor=(0, 1), color="w")
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.cursor_label)
        
        self.v_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=90, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.h_line = pg.InfiniteLine(angle=0, movable=False, pen=pg.mkPen('w', style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.v_line)
        self.map_plot.addItem(self.h_line)
        
        self.map_plot.scene().sigMouseMoved.connect(self.on_map_mouse_move)

        layout.addWidget(self.map_plot)
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Power Map")

    def init_evolution_tab(self):
        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # data prep
        laps = np.arange(2, len(self.results_list) + 1)
        subset = self.results_list[1:]
        mus_x = [r.mu_x for r in subset]
        mus_y = [r.mu_y for r in subset]
        regens = [r.regen_eff for r in subset]
        lap_times = [r.t[-1] - r.t[0] for r in subset]
        
        # 1. grip evolution
        self.plot_grip = pg.PlotWidget(title="Grip evolution (Mu)")
        self.plot_grip.setLabel('left', 'Friction coeff [-]')
        self.plot_grip.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        self.plot_grip.addLegend()

        self.plot_grip.plot(laps, mus_x, pen='r', symbol='o', name="Mu X (Long)") 
        self.plot_grip.plot(laps, mus_y, pen='b', symbol='t', name="Mu Y (Lat)")
        
        self.grip_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_grip.addItem(self.grip_marker)

        # 2. regen evolution
        self.plot_regen = pg.PlotWidget(title="Regen Efficiency")
        self.plot_regen.setLabel('left', 'Efficiency [-]')
        self.plot_regen.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        
        self.plot_regen.plot(laps, regens, pen='g', symbol='s', name="Regen Eff")
        
        self.regen_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_regen.addItem(self.regen_marker)

        # 3. lap time evolution
        self.plot_times = pg.PlotWidget(title="Lap Time Evolution")
        self.plot_times.setLabel('left', 'Time [s]')
        self.plot_times.setLabel('bottom', 'Lap Number')
        self.plot_times.showGrid(x=False, y=False)
        
        self.plot_times.plot(laps, lap_times, pen='c', symbol='d', name="Lap Time")
        
        self.time_marker = pg.InfiniteLine(pos=1, angle=90, pen=pg.mkPen('b', width=2, style=Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
        self.plot_times.addItem(self.time_marker)

        # link axes
        self.plot_grip.setXLink(self.plot_times)
        self.plot_regen.setXLink(self.plot_times)

        layout.addWidget(self.plot_grip)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_regen)
        layout.addWidget(self.plot_times)
        
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.tabs.addTab(widget, "Evolution")

    def update_view(self):
        res = self.current_res
        
        # update summary labels
        lap_duration = res.t[-1] - res.t[0]
        energy_used = res.E[-1] - res.E[0]
        max_v = np.max(res.v) * 3.6
        avg_v = np.mean(res.v) * 3.6
        total_energy = res.E[-1]

        self.lbl_lap_time.setText(f"{lap_duration:.3f}")
        self.lbl_energy.setText(f"{energy_used:.3f}")
        self.lbl_max_v.setText(f"{max_v:.1f}")
        self.lbl_avg_v.setText(f"{avg_v:.1f}")
        self.lbl_soc.setText(f"{total_energy:.3f}")
        self.setWindowTitle(f"Endurance results - Lap {self.current_idx + 1}")

        # data prep for plots (relative to lap start)
        s_rel = res.s - res.s[0]
        t_rel = res.t - res.t[0]
        min_len = min(len(res.f_acc), len(res.v))
        s_plot = s_rel[:min_len]
        v_plot = res.v[:min_len]
        
        # update dynamic plots
        self.curve_v.setData(s_rel, res.v * 3.6)
        self.curve_t.setData(s_rel, t_rel)
        
        power_kw = ((res.f_acc[:min_len] + res.f_brake[:min_len]) * v_plot) / 1000.0
        acc_mask = power_kw > 0
        reg_mask = power_kw <= 0
        
        p_acc = np.copy(power_kw)
        p_acc[~acc_mask] = 0
        p_reg = np.copy(power_kw)
        p_reg[~reg_mask] = 0
        
        self.curve_acc.setData(s_plot, p_acc)
        self.curve_reg.setData(s_plot, p_reg)
        self.curve_e.setData(s_rel, res.E) 
        self.curve_f_acc.setData(s_plot, res.f_acc[:min_len])
        self.curve_f_brake.setData(s_plot, res.f_brake[:min_len])

        # update evolution markers
        current_lap_num = self.current_idx + 1
        if hasattr(self, 'grip_marker'):
            self.grip_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)
        if hasattr(self, 'regen_marker'):
            self.regen_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)
        if hasattr(self, 'time_marker'):
            self.time_marker.setPos(current_lap_num)

        # update map
        self.update_map_data(s_rel, v_plot, power_kw)

    def update_map_data(self, s_rel, v_plot, power_kw):
        x_plot = np.interp(s_rel, self.track.s, self.track.x)
        y_plot = np.interp(s_rel, self.track.s, self.track.y)
        
        n = len(power_kw)
        x_plot = x_plot[:n]
        y_plot = y_plot[:n]
        s_plot = s_rel[:n]

        self.map_data = {
            'x': x_plot, 'y': y_plot, 'p': power_kw, 'v': v_plot * 3.6, 's': s_plot
        }

        # coloring logic
        p_min, p_max = np.min(power_kw), np.max(power_kw)
        span = p_max - p_min + 1e-9
        zero_pos = np.clip((0 - p_min) / span, 0.0, 1.0)

        pos = np.array([0.0, zero_pos, 1.0])
        color_def = np.array([
            [255, 0, 0, 255], [0, 150, 255, 255], [0, 255, 0, 255]
        ], dtype=np.ubyte)
        cmap = pg.ColorMap(pos, color_def)
        
        norm_power = (power_kw - p_min) / span
        brushes = cmap.map(norm_power, mode='qcolor')

        self.scatter.setData(x=x_plot, y=y_plot, brush=brushes)
        self.start_marker.setData(x=[x_plot[0]], y=[y_plot[0]])

    def on_map_mouse_move(self, pos):
        if self.map_plot.sceneBoundingRect().contains(pos):
            mouse_point = self.map_plot.plotItem.vb.mapSceneToView(pos)
            mx, my = mouse_point.x(), mouse_point.y()
            
            # find closest point
            dists = np.sqrt((self.map_data['x'] - mx)**2 + (self.map_data['y'] - my)**2)
            idx = np.argmin(dists)
            
            if dists[idx] < 20.0:
                x, y = self.map_data['x'][idx], self.map_data['y'][idx]
                p, v, s = self.map_data['p'][idx], self.map_data['v'][idx], self.map_data['s'][idx]
                
                self.v_line.setPos(x)
                self.h_line.setPos(y)
                
                color_hex = "#00ff00" if p > 0 else "#ff5555" if p < 0 else "#ffffff"
                html = (f"<div style='background-color: rgba(0,0,0,150); padding: 5px; font-size: 14px;'>"
                        f"<b>Dist: {s:.1f} m</b><br>Speed: {v:.1f} km/h<br>"
                        f"Power: <b style='color: {color_hex};'>{p:.1f} kW</b></div>")
                
                self.cursor_label.setHtml(html)
                self.cursor_label.setPos(x, y)
                self.cursor_label.show()
                self.v_line.show()
                self.h_line.show()
            else:
                self.cursor_label.hide()
                self.v_line.hide()
                self.h_line.hide()

    def on_save_clicked(self):
        now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M")
        default_name = f"{self.track.name}_Endurance_{now_str}.npz"
        if not os.path.exists("results"): os.makedirs("results")
        
        filepath, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Save Endurance Results", 
                                                  os.path.join("results", default_name), 
                                                  "NumPy Zip (*.npz)")
        
        if filepath:
            try:
                # currently saving only the selected lap
                save_results_to_npz(filepath, self.current_res, self.track.name, self.ds)
                self.btn_save.setText("Saved Current Lap!")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
