import numpy as np
import casadi as ca
from PyQt6.QtWidgets import (QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, 
                             QSlider, QLabel, QMessageBox, QPushButton, 
                             QDoubleSpinBox, QTabWidget, QGroupBox, QApplication,QSizePolicy)
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt
from PyQt6.QtCore import pyqtSignal
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qtagg import FigureCanvasQTAgg, NavigationToolbar2QT
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
from src.ui.loader import LoadingOverlay
from scipy.ndimage import median_filter

class MapTab(QWidget):
 
    def __init__(self, map_data, fixed_axis='soc', parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        #self.setStyleSheet("background-color: white; color: black;")
        self.map_data = map_data
        self.fixed_axis = fixed_axis
        
        
        self.laptime_map = map_data['interpolant']
        self.z_min = map_data['z_min']
        self.z_max = map_data['z_max']
        
        # --- UI Layout ---
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self) 
        
        # Matplotlib Canvas 
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(8, 6), dpi=100)
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
                
        # Horizontal layout
        content_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.canvas.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding)
        content_layout.addWidget(self.canvas, stretch=1)
        
        # --- Slider 
        slider_layout = QVBoxLayout() 
        self.lbl_slider = QLabel("")
        self.lbl_slider.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter) 
        
        # Vertical slider
        self.slider = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Vertical)
        self.slider.setTickPosition(QSlider.TickPosition.TicksRight) 
        
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            self.slider.setMinimum(10)
            self.slider.setMaximum(100)
            self.slider.setTickInterval(10)
            self.slider.setValue(50)
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh = np.meshgrid(map_data['e_fine'], map_data['r_fine'])
        else:
            self.slider.setMinimum(int(map_data['r_min'] * 100))
            self.slider.setMaximum(100)
            self.slider.setTickInterval(10)
            self.slider.setValue(int(map_data['r_max'] * 50))
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh = np.meshgrid(map_data['e_fine'], map_data['soc_fine'])

        slider_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_slider)
        slider_layout.addWidget(self.slider)
        
        
        content_layout.addLayout(slider_layout)
      
        main_layout.addLayout(content_layout)
        
        # Camera default view
        self.ax3d = None
        self.view_elev = 20
        self.view_azim = 30
        
        self.slider.valueChanged.connect(self.on_slider_changed)
        self.on_slider_changed(self.slider.value()) 

    def on_slider_changed(self, val):
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            self.lbl_slider.setText(f"SoC: {val} %")
        else:
            self.lbl_slider.setText(f"Regen: {val/100.0:.2f}")
        self.update_plot()

    def update_plot(self):
        if self.fixed_axis == 'soc':
            soc_val = self.slider.value() / 100.0
            # [SoC, Energy, Regen]
            pts_casadi = np.vstack((
                np.full_like(self.X_mesh.ravel(), soc_val),
                self.X_mesh.ravel(),
                self.Y_mesh.ravel()
            ))
            x_label, y_label = 'Energy target [kWh]', 'Regen [%]'
            title = f'Laptime map (SoC = {soc_val*100:.0f} %)'
        else:
            regen_val = self.slider.value() / 100.0
            # [SoC, Energy, Regen]
            pts_casadi = np.vstack((
                self.Y_mesh.ravel(),
                self.X_mesh.ravel(),
                np.full_like(self.X_mesh.ravel(), regen_val)
            ))
            x_label, y_label = 'Energy target [kWh]', 'SoC [%]'
            title = f'Laptime map (Regen = {regen_val:.2f})'

        Z_laptime = np.array(self.laptime_map(pts_casadi)).reshape(self.X_mesh.shape)

        if self.ax3d is not None:
            self.view_elev = self.ax3d.elev
            self.view_azim = self.ax3d.azim

        self.fig.clear()
        self.ax3d = self.fig.add_subplot(1, 1, 1, projection='3d')
        
        surf = self.ax3d.plot_surface(
            self.X_mesh, self.Y_mesh*100, Z_laptime, 
            cmap='viridis_r', edgecolor='none', alpha=0.9,
            vmin=self.z_min, vmax=self.z_max
        )
        
        self.ax3d.set_zlim(self.z_min, self.z_max)
        self.ax3d.set_xlabel(x_label)
        self.ax3d.set_ylabel(y_label)
        self.ax3d.set_zlabel('Lap time [s]')
        self.ax3d.set_title(title)
        
        #self.fig.colorbar(surf, ax=self.ax3d, shrink=0.5, aspect=10, label='Lap time [s]')
        self.ax3d.view_init(elev=self.view_elev, azim=self.view_azim)
        
        self.fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.05, right=0.85, bottom=0.05, top=0.95)
        self.canvas.draw_idle()


class LaptimeAppMainWindow(QWidget):
    optimization_requested = pyqtSignal(float, float,float,float, object)
    deployment_requested = pyqtSignal(float, float, float,float, object)

    def __init__(self, filepath, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Endurance strategy")
    
        self.loading_overlay = LoadingOverlay(self)
        self.map_data = self.load_data(filepath)
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        self.showMaximized()
        
        control_group = QGroupBox("Optimization settings")
        control_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        # Button
        self.btn_optimize = QPushButton("Calculate opt energy map")
        self.btn_optimize.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; background-color: #0d16d1; color: white; padding: 5px;")
        self.btn_optimize.clicked.connect(self.run_optimization)

        self.btn_deploy = QPushButton("Calculate opt deployment for given input")
        self.btn_deploy.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; background-color: #2ca02c; color: white; padding: 5px;")
        self.btn_deploy.clicked.connect(self.run_deployment)
        
        # Inputs
        lbl_regen = QLabel("Recuperation [%]:")
        self.spin_regen = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.spin_regen.setRange(0.0, 100)
        self.spin_regen.setSingleStep(1)
        self.spin_regen.setValue(80) 
        
        lbl_delta = QLabel("Time delta [%]:")
        self.spin_delta = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.spin_delta.setRange(0, 10.0)
        self.spin_delta.setSingleStep(0.5)
        self.spin_delta.setValue(0.0)

        
        control_layout.addWidget(self.btn_optimize)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.btn_deploy)
        control_layout.addStretch() 
        control_layout.addWidget(lbl_regen)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.spin_regen)
        control_layout.addSpacing(20)
        control_layout.addWidget(lbl_delta)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.spin_delta)
        
        control_group.setLayout(control_layout)
        main_layout.addWidget(control_group)
        
     
        self.tabs = QTabWidget()
        
        if self.map_data:
            #creting tabs
            self.tab_soc = MapTab(self.map_data, fixed_axis='soc')
            self.tab_regen = MapTab(self.map_data, fixed_axis='regen')
            
            side_by_side_widget = QWidget()
            side_by_side_widget.setStyleSheet("background-color: white; color: black;")
            side_by_side_layout = QHBoxLayout(side_by_side_widget)
            side_by_side_layout.addWidget(self.tab_soc)
            side_by_side_layout.addWidget(self.tab_regen)
            
            
            self.tabs.addTab(side_by_side_widget, "Laptime maps")

        else:
            self.tabs.addTab(QLabel("Data se nepodařilo načíst."), "Error")
            
        main_layout.addWidget(self.tabs)

    def load_data(self, filepath):
        try:
            data = np.load(filepath)
            soc_targets = data['soc_targets']
            e_targets = data['e_targets']
            regen_targets = data['regen_targets']
            flat_lap_times = data['flat_lap_times']
            
            shape_3d = (len(soc_targets), len(e_targets), len(regen_targets))
            lap_times_3d = flat_lap_times.reshape(shape_3d)
            
            # --- ZDE ZAČÍNÁ FILTRACE OUTLIERŮ ---
            
            # Vytvoříme vyhlazenou mapu pomocí okna 3x3x3
            # Parametr 'size=3' znamená, že se průměruje buňka a její bezprostřední sousedé
            smoothed_3d = median_filter(lap_times_3d, size=3)
            
            # Vypočítáme absolutní rozdíl mezi původními daty a mediánem okolí
            diff = np.abs(lap_times_3d - smoothed_3d)
            
            # Nastavíme práh (threshold). 
            # Pokud se bod liší od svého okolí o více než 5 sekund, považujeme ho za selhání solveru.
            outlier_threshold = 5.0 
            
            outliers_mask = diff > outlier_threshold
            num_outliers = np.sum(outliers_mask)
            
            if num_outliers > 0:
                print(f"[Zpracování dat] Nalezeno a opraveno {num_outliers} outlierů pomocí mediánového filtru.")
                # Přepíšeme nesmyslné hodnoty hodnotami z vyhlazené matice
                lap_times_3d[outliers_mask] = smoothed_3d[outliers_mask]
            # --- KONEC FILTRACE ---
            
            # Pokračujeme v původním kódu - připravíme pole pro CasADi
            flat_for_casadi = lap_times_3d.ravel(order='F')
            
            grid_axes = [soc_targets.tolist(), e_targets.tolist(), regen_targets.tolist()]
            interpolant = ca.interpolant('laptime_map', 'bspline', grid_axes, flat_for_casadi.tolist())

            self.min_e_target = min(e_targets)
            self.max_e_target = max(e_targets)
            
            e_fine = np.linspace(min(e_targets), max(e_targets), 50) 
            r_fine = np.linspace(min(regen_targets), 1.0, 50)
            soc_fine = np.linspace(min(soc_targets), 1.0, 50)
            
            return {
                'interpolant': interpolant,
                'e_fine': e_fine,
                'r_fine': r_fine,
                'soc_fine': soc_fine,
                'r_min': min(regen_targets),
                'r_max': max(regen_targets),
                'z_min': np.nanmin(lap_times_3d), # Použijeme už vyfiltrovanou matici pro min/max
                'z_max': np.nanmax(lap_times_3d)  # Jinak by se ti zde uložilo těch chybných 120+ s
            }
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Chyba načítání", f"Nepodařilo se načíst mapu z {filepath}:\n{e}")
            return None
        
 
    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        self.loading_overlay.resize(self.size())
        super().resizeEvent(event)


    def add_result_tab(self, payload):
        """
        Zpracovává payloady z workeru. Rozlišuje, zda jde o celou 3D mapu, nebo jen deployment.
        """
        # --- Zpracování 3D Mapy (Tlačítko 1) ---
        if payload.kind == "endurance_map": 
            has_opt_energy_tab = False
            for i in range(self.tabs.count()):
                if self.tabs.tabText(i) == "Optimal energy map":
                    has_opt_energy_tab = True
                    self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(i) # Pokud existuje, jen se na ni přepneme
                    
            if not has_opt_energy_tab:
                opt_energy_tab = OptimalEnergyMapTab(payload, self)
                self.tabs.addTab(opt_energy_tab, "Optimal energy map")
                self.tabs.setCurrentWidget(opt_energy_tab)

        # --- Zpracování konkrétního deploymentu (Tlačítko 2) ---
        elif payload.kind == "endurance_deployment":
            result_count = sum(1 for i in range(self.tabs.count()) if self.tabs.tabText(i).startswith("Deployment #"))
            
            result_tab = StrategyResultTab(payload, self)
            tab_name = f"Deployment #{result_count + 1}"
            self.tabs.addTab(result_tab, tab_name)
            self.tabs.setCurrentWidget(result_tab)

    def run_optimization(self):
      
        # 1. Nejdříve zkontrolujeme, zda už tab "Optimal energy map" existuje
        for i in range(self.tabs.count()):
            if self.tabs.tabText(i) == "Optimal energy map":
                # Tab existuje! Jen se na něj přepneme a funkci rovnou ukončíme (zabráníme spuštění simulace)
                self.tabs.setCurrentIndex(i)
                return
            
        regen_val = self.spin_regen.value()/100
        delta_val = self.spin_delta.value()/100
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Calculating optimal strategy...", indefinite=True,progress_text_fmt="Solving grid points ({} / {})")
        self.optimization_requested.emit(regen_val, delta_val,self.min_e_target,self.max_e_target,self.map_data)

    def run_deployment(self):
        regen_val = self.spin_regen.value()/100
        delta_val = self.spin_delta.value()/100
        # Zde použijeme točící se GIF, protože jde jen o jeden konkrétní bod, takže to bude hned
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Calculating optimum energy deployment for given input", indefinite=True)
        self.deployment_requested.emit(regen_val, delta_val,self.min_e_target,self.max_e_target,self.map_data)
        
        

    def stop_loading(self):
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()

class StrategyResultTab(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, payload, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        
        # Rozbalení obou výsledků ze solveru
        self.res_opt = payload.data.get("opt_soc", {})
        self.res_full = payload.data.get("full_soc", {})
        self.meta = payload.meta
        
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # Kontrola, zda prošla alespoň ta hlavní optimalizace
        if not self.res_opt.get("success", False):
            error_lbl = QLabel("Optimization failed for these specific inputs. Try different values.")
            error_lbl.setStyleSheet("color: red; font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold;")
            main_layout.addWidget(error_lbl)
            return

        # --- 1. Výpočty pro textové shrnutí ---
        summary_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        total_time = self.res_opt['total_time']
        points = self.res_opt['points']
        total_energy = sum(self.res_opt['e_targets'])
        soc_start = self.res_opt['soc_start']
        points = self.res_opt['points']
        total_energy = sum(self.res_opt['e_targets'])

        has_full_soc = self.res_full.get("success", False)
        
        if has_full_soc:
            # Rozdíly: Opt - Full
            time_saved = self.res_full['total_time'] - total_time   # Kladné = ušetřili jsme čas
            points_diff = points - self.res_full['points']          # Kladné = získali jsme body
            energy_diff = total_energy - sum(self.res_full['e_targets']) # Záporné = spálili jsme méně energie
            
            # Nastavení barev (Zelená pro zlepšení, Červená pro zhoršení)
            c_time = "#2ca02c" if time_saved >= 0 else "#d62728"
            c_pts = "#2ca02c" if points_diff >= 0 else "#d62728"
            c_eng = "#2ca02c" if energy_diff <= 0 else "#d62728" # U energie je menší = lepší
            
            info_text = (
                f"<span style='color:#1f77b4;'><b>Opt. SoC Start:</b> {soc_start * 100:.1f} %</span> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Points:</b> {points:.2f} (<span style='color:{c_pts};'>{'+' if points_diff > 0 else ''}{points_diff:.2f}</span>) &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Energy:</b> {total_energy:.3f} kWh (<span style='color:{c_eng};'>{'+' if energy_diff > 0 else ''}{energy_diff:.3f}</span>) &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Time:</b> {total_time:.2f} s (<span style='color:{c_time};'>{'-' if time_saved > 0 else '+'}{abs(time_saved):.2f} s</span>)"
            )
        else:
            # Fallback text, kdyby srovnávací "Full SoC" solver z nějakého důvodu selhal
            info_text = (
                f"<span style='color:#1f77b4;'><b>Opt. SoC Start:</b> {soc_start * 100:.1f} %</span> &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Total points:</b> {points:.2f} &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Total Energy used:</b> {total_energy:.3f} kWh &nbsp;&nbsp;|&nbsp;&nbsp; "
                f"<b>Opt. total time:</b> {total_time:.2f} s"
            )
        
        lbl_summary = QLabel(info_text)
        lbl_summary.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 15px; 
            padding: 12px; 
            background-color: #05001f; 
            color: white;
            border: 1px solid #444;
            border-radius: 6px;
        """)
        lbl_summary.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        summary_layout.addWidget(lbl_summary)
        main_layout.addLayout(summary_layout)
        
        # --- 2. Matplotlib grafy ---
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(10, 8), dpi=100) 
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
        self.toolbar = NavigationToolbar2QT(self.canvas, self)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.toolbar)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.canvas)
        
        self.plot_results()

    def plot_results(self):
        self.fig.clear()
        
        laps = np.arange(1, len(self.res_opt['lap_times']) + 1)
        
        # Data z optimální strategie
        opt_lap_times = np.array(self.res_opt['lap_times'])
        opt_e_targets = np.array(self.res_opt['e_targets'])
        
        # Ověříme, jestli máme data i z porovnávací "Full SoC" simulace
        has_full_soc = self.res_full.get("success", False)
        if has_full_soc:
            full_lap_times = np.array(self.res_full['lap_times'])
            full_e_targets = np.array(self.res_full['e_targets'])

        # ==========================================
        # GRAF 1: Lap Times (Nahoře)
        # ==========================================
        ax1 = self.fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 1)
        
        ax1.plot(laps, opt_lap_times, marker='o', color='#d62728', linewidth=2.5, label='Opt. SoC Start')
        
        if has_full_soc:
            ax1.plot(laps, full_lap_times, marker='x', color='gray', linewidth=2, linestyle='--', label='100% SoC Start')
        
        ax1.set_ylabel('Lap time [s]', color='#d62728')
        ax1.tick_params(axis='y', labelcolor='#d62728')
        ax1.set_xticks(laps)
        ax1.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
        ax1.set_title('Laptimes')
        ax1.legend()

        # ==========================================
        # GRAF 2: Energy Target (Dole)
        # ==========================================
        ax2 = self.fig.add_subplot(2, 1, 2, sharex=ax1)
        
        ax2.plot(laps, opt_e_targets, marker='s', color='#1f77b4', linewidth=2.5, markersize=6, label='Opt. SoC Start')
        
        if has_full_soc:
            ax2.plot(laps, full_e_targets, marker='^', color='lightblue', linewidth=2, linestyle='--', label='100% SoC Start')
        
        ax2.set_xlabel('Lap number', fontweight='bold')
        ax2.set_ylabel('Energy target [kWh]', color='#1f77b4')
        ax2.tick_params(axis='y', labelcolor='#1f77b4')
        ax2.grid(True, linestyle='--', alpha=0.5)
        ax2.set_title('Energy deployment strategy')
        ax2.legend()

        self.fig.tight_layout()
        self.canvas.draw()


class OptimalEnergyMapTab(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, solver_res, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.meta = solver_res.meta
        
        # Načteme NAŠE NOVÉ interpolanty a limity z payloadu
        self.e_interpolant = self.meta['e_interpolant']
        self.soc_interpolant = self.meta['soc_interpolant']
        self.grid_regen = self.meta['grid_regen']
        self.grid_delta = self.meta['grid_delta']
        self.z_min = self.meta['z_min_e']
        self.z_max = self.meta['z_max_e']
        
        # --- UI Layout (Stejný jako minule) ---
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(8, 6), dpi=100)
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.fig)
        self.toolbar = NavigationToolbar2QT(self.canvas, self)
        
        control_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        sliders_vbox = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # Slidery
        regen_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_regen = QLabel("Regen: 0 %")
        self.lbl_regen.setFixedWidth(100)
        self.slider_regen = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        self.slider_regen.setMinimum(int(min(self.grid_regen) * 100))
        self.slider_regen.setMaximum(100)
        self.slider_regen.setValue(int(max(self.grid_regen) * 50))
        regen_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_regen)
        regen_layout.addWidget(self.slider_regen)
        
        delta_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.lbl_delta = QLabel("Delta: 0 %")
        self.lbl_delta.setFixedWidth(100)
        self.slider_delta = QSlider(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)
        self.slider_delta.setMinimum(int(min(self.grid_delta) * 100))
        self.slider_delta.setMaximum(int(max(self.grid_delta) * 100))
        self.slider_delta.setValue(0)
        delta_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_delta)
        delta_layout.addWidget(self.slider_delta)
        
        sliders_vbox.addLayout(regen_layout)
        sliders_vbox.addLayout(delta_layout)
        
        # ZMĚNA: Přidán prostor pro výpis Optimálního SoC!
        self.lbl_opt_energy = QLabel("Optimal energy:\n-- kWh\n\nOptimal SoC start:\n-- %")
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setStyleSheet("""
            font-size: 15px; 
            font-weight: bold; 
            color: #1f77b4; 
            background-color: #f8f9fa; 
            padding: 10px; 
            border: 2px solid #1f77b4;
            border-radius: 5px;
        """)
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        control_layout.addLayout(sliders_vbox)
        control_layout.addSpacing(20)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.lbl_opt_energy)
        
        main_layout.addWidget(self.toolbar)
        main_layout.addLayout(control_layout) 
        main_layout.addWidget(self.canvas, stretch=1) 
        
        self.ax3d = None
        self.view_elev = 20  
        self.view_azim = 38
        self.point_plot = None 
        
        self.plot_optimal_surface()
        
        self.slider_regen.valueChanged.connect(self.update_point)
        self.slider_delta.valueChanged.connect(self.update_point)
        
        self.update_point()

    def plot_optimal_surface(self):
        # Nyní tvoříme jen 2D MeshGrid (Regen a Delta)
        r_fine = np.linspace(min(self.grid_regen), max(self.grid_regen), 50)
        d_fine = np.linspace(min(self.grid_delta), max(self.grid_delta), 50)

        R_mesh, D_mesh = np.meshgrid(r_fine, d_fine, indexing='ij')
        pts_scipy = np.column_stack((R_mesh.ravel(), D_mesh.ravel()))
        
        # Místo argmax rovnou vyhodnotíme e_interpolant
        Z_opt_energy = self.e_interpolant(pts_scipy).reshape(R_mesh.shape)
        
        self.fig.clear()
        self.ax3d = self.fig.add_subplot(1, 1, 1, projection='3d')
        
        surf = self.ax3d.plot_surface(
            R_mesh * 100, D_mesh * 100, Z_opt_energy, 
            cmap='plasma', edgecolor='none', alpha=0.9,
            vmin=self.z_min, vmax=self.z_max
        )
        
        self.ax3d.set_xlabel('Recuperation percentage [%]')
        self.ax3d.set_ylabel('Time delta [%]')
        self.ax3d.set_zlabel('Opt energy target [kWh]')
        self.ax3d.set_title('Optimal energy target')
        
        self.fig.colorbar(surf, ax=self.ax3d, shrink=0.5, aspect=10, label='Energy target [kWh]')
        self.ax3d.view_init(elev=self.view_elev, azim=self.view_azim)
        self.fig.subplots_adjust(left=0.05, right=0.85, bottom=0.05, top=0.95)

    def update_point(self):
        regen_val = self.slider_regen.value() / 100.0
        delta_val = self.slider_delta.value() / 100.0
        
        self.lbl_regen.setText(f"Regen: {regen_val*100:.0f} %")
        self.lbl_delta.setText(f"Delta: {delta_val*100:.0f} %")

        # ROVNOU přečteme hodnoty pro zadaný regen a deltu ze 2D interpolantů
        # ([0] je nutné, protože interpolant vrací pole výsledků o délce 1)
        opt_e = float(self.e_interpolant([regen_val, delta_val])[0])
        opt_soc = float(self.soc_interpolant([regen_val, delta_val])[0])
        
        # Aktualizace textu s OBĚMA proměnnými
        self.lbl_opt_energy.setText(f"Optimal Energy:\n{opt_e:.3f} kWh\n\nOptimal SoC Start:\n{opt_soc*100:.1f} %")

        if self.point_plot is not None:
            self.point_plot.remove()
            self.point_plot = None
            
        if self.ax3d is not None and not np.isnan(opt_e):
            self.point_plot, = self.ax3d.plot(
                [regen_val * 100], [delta_val * 100], [opt_e],
                marker='o', color='cyan', markersize=12, markeredgecolor='black', zorder=10
            )
            
            self.canvas.draw_idle()

from PyQt6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QProgressBar
from PyQt6.QtCore import Qt, QSize
from PyQt6.QtGui import QPalette, QColor, QMovie

class LoadingOverlay(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WidgetAttribute.WA_TransparentForMouseEvents, False) 
        self.hide()
        
        self.setAutoFillBackground(True)
        palette = self.palette()
        # Poloprůhledné černé pozadí
        palette.setColor(QPalette.ColorRole.Window, QColor(0, 0, 0, 200))
        self.setPalette(palette)
        
        # Layout
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        layout.setSpacing(20) # Mezera mezi GIFem a textem
        
        # --- 1. GIF LABEL ---
        self.lbl_gif = QLabel()
        self.lbl_gif.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
     
        gif_path = "data/vejsi_loading.gif" 
        self.movie = QMovie(gif_path)
     
        self.movie.setScaledSize(QSize(300, 300)) 
        self.movie.finished.connect(self.movie.start)
        self.lbl_gif.setMovie(self.movie)
        
        # --- 2. TEXT LABEL ---
        self.lbl_text = QLabel("Calculating...")
        self.lbl_text.setStyleSheet("color: white; font-size: 18px; font-weight: bold;")
        self.lbl_text.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        
        # --- 3. PROGRESS BAR 
        self.progress = QProgressBar()
        self.progress.setFixedWidth(300)
        self.progress.setTextVisible(True)
        self.progress.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.progress.setStyleSheet("""
            QProgressBar {
                border: 2px solid grey;
                border-radius: 5px;
                background-color: #333;
                color: white;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
            QProgressBar::chunk {
                background-color: #007bff;
                width: 20px; 
            }
        """)
        
        # Přidání do layoutu
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_gif)   # GIF nahoře
        layout.addWidget(self.lbl_text)  # Text uprostřed
        layout.addWidget(self.progress)  # Bar dole
        
        self.setLayout(layout)
        
        self.current_progress_fmt = None

    def show_loading(self, text="Calculating...", indefinite=True, progress_text_fmt=None):
        """
        Zobrazí overlay.
        Args:
            text: Úvodní text.
            indefinite: 
                True = Single Lap / ACP -> Ukáže GIF, skryje Bar.
                False = Endurance -> Ukáže Bar, skryje GIF.
        """
        if self.parent():
            self.resize(self.parent().size())
            
        self.lbl_text.setText(text)
        
        # Uložení formátu textu
        if progress_text_fmt:
            self.current_progress_fmt = progress_text_fmt
        else:
            self.current_progress_fmt = f"{text} ({{}} / {{}})"

        if indefinite:
            # --- REŽIM GIF (Točící se kolečko / Animace) ---
            self.progress.hide()       # Schováme progress bar
            self.lbl_gif.show()        # Ukážeme GIF
            self.movie.start()         # Spustíme animaci
        else:
            # --- REŽIM PROGRESS BAR (Endurance) ---
            self.lbl_gif.hide()        # Schováme GIF (nebo ho necháme, pokud chceš obojí)
            self.movie.stop()          # Zastavíme animaci (šetří CPU)
            
            self.progress.show()       # Ukážeme progress bar
            self.progress.setRange(0, 100)
            self.progress.setValue(0)

        self.show()
        self.raise_()

    def update_progress(self, current, total):
        # ... (Tato metoda zůstává stejná jako předtím) ...
        if self.current_progress_fmt:
            try:
                self.lbl_text.setText(self.current_progress_fmt.format(current, total))
            except:
                self.lbl_text.setText(f"{current} / {total}")
        
        if self.progress.minimum() == 0 and self.progress.maximum() == 0:
            self.progress.setRange(0, total)
            self.progress.setTextVisible(True)
            self.progress.setFormat("%v / %m") 
            
        self.progress.setValue(current)

    def hide_loading(self):
        self.movie.stop() # Důležité: zastavit GIF při skrytí
        self.hide()

class MplCanvas(FigureCanvasQTAgg):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, width=5, height=4, dpi=100):
        
        self.fig = Figure(figsize=(width, height), dpi=dpi)
        self.axes = self.fig.add_subplot(111)
        
        super(MplCanvas, self).__init__(self.fig)
        self.setParent(parent)
        
       
        FigureCanvasQTAgg.updateGeometry(self)

    def init_track_section(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Track Selection")
        layout = QFormLayout()

        self.combo_track = QComboBox()
        self.combo_track.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_track_changed)

        group.setLayout(layout)

    def init_car_params_section(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Car Parameters")
        layout = QFormLayout()

        self.combo_model = QComboBox()
        self.combo_model.addItem("Mass point (Euler)", "mass_point")
        self.combo_model.addItem("Mass point (Rk4)", "mass_point_rk4")
        self.combo_model.addItem("Single track", "single_track")

    
        layout.addRow("Vehicle model:", self.combo_model)

        def add_param(label, val, unit, min_val=0.0, max_val=10000.0, step=0.1):
            spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin.setRange(min_val, max_val)
            spin.setValue(val)
            spin.setSingleStep(step)
            spin.setSuffix(f" {unit}")
            layout.addRow(label, spin)
            return spin

        self.input_mass = add_param("Mass:", 280, "kg", min_val=150, step=0.5)
        self.input_cd = add_param("Drag Coeff (Cd):", 1.83, "", step=0.01)
        self.input_cl = add_param("Downforce Coeff (Cl):", 5.64, "", step=0.01)
        self.input_mu_x = add_param("Friction coeff x:", 1.25, "", step=0.01)
        self.input_mu_y = add_param("Friction coeff y:", 1.25, "", step=0.01)
        self.input_ds = add_param("Sim distance step:", 0.5, "m", min_val=0.1, max_val=2.0, step=0.05)
        
        # Single Lap Energy Limit
        self.check_energy_limit = QCheckBox("Enable Energy Limit")
        self.check_energy_limit.setChecked(False) 
        self.input_limit_e = add_param("Energy limit", 0.2, "kWh", min_val=0.05, step=0.01)
        self.input_limit_e.setEnabled(False)

        self.check_energy_limit.toggled.connect(self.input_limit_e.setEnabled)
        layout.addRow(self.check_energy_limit, self.input_limit_e)
        self.check_drs = QCheckBox("Enable DRS")
        self.check_drs.setChecked(True) 
        layout.addRow(self.check_drs)

        group.setLayout(layout)
        self.control_layout.addWidget(group)

    def init_endurance_section(self):
        group = QGroupBox("Endurance Parameters")
        layout = QFormLayout()

        def add_dspin(val, unit, min_val, max_val, step):
            spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
            spin.setRange(min_val, max_val)
            spin.setValue(val)
            spin.setSingleStep(step)
            spin.setSuffix(f" {unit}")
            return spin

        self.input_battery_cap = add_dspin(6.0, "kWh", 1.0, 20.0, 0.05)
        layout.addRow("Battery Capacity:", self.input_battery_cap)

        self.input_laps = QSpinBox()
        self.input_laps.setRange(1, 200)
        self.input_laps.setValue(22) 
        self.input_laps.setSuffix(" laps")
        layout.addRow("Number of Laps:", self.input_laps)

        # Endurance Run Button
        self.btn_run_endurance = QPushButton("Run endurance")
        self.btn_run_endurance.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_run_endurance.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton { background-color: #198754; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 10px; margin-top: 5px; }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #157347; }
        """)
        self.btn_run_endurance.clicked.connect(self.on_run_endurance_clicked)
        layout.addRow(self.btn_run_endurance)

        # Endurance Strategy Button
        self.btn_endurance_strategy = QPushButton("Endurance strategy")
        self.btn_endurance_strategy.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.btn_endurance_strategy.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton { background-color: #17a2b8; color: white; font-weight: bold; padding: 10px; margin-top: 5px; border-radius: 4px; }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #138496; }
        """)
        self.btn_endurance_strategy.clicked.connect(self.on_endurance_strategy_clicked)
        layout.addRow(self.btn_endurance_strategy)

        # ACP Test Button
        self.btn_acp_test = QPushButton("Run laptime sensitivity test")
        self.btn_acp_test.setStyleSheet("background-color: #6c757d; color: white; padding: 5px; margin-top: 5px;")
        self.btn_acp_test.clicked.connect(self.on_acp_test_clicked)
        layout.addRow(self.btn_acp_test)

        group.setLayout(layout)
        self.control_layout.addWidget(group)

    def create_solver(self,car, track, laps=1):
        """
        Creates the correct solver based on car configuration.
        """
        if car.model_type == "single_track":
            return SingleTrackSolver(car, track, laps=laps)
            
        elif car.model_type == "mass_point":
            return MassPointSolver(car, track,rk4=False, laps=laps)
        
        elif car.model_type == "mass_point_rk4":
            return MassPointSolver(car, track,rk4=True, laps=laps)
            
        else:
            # Fallback
            print(f"⚠️ Unknown model type '{car.model_type}', defaulting to MassPoint.")
            return MassPointSolver(car, track, laps=laps)

    # ==========================================
    # DATA LOADING & TRACK PLOTTING
    # ==========================================

    def load_track_list(self):
        """Searches 'data/tracks_render' for files."""
        track_dir = "data/tracks"
        if not os.path.exists(track_dir):
            os.makedirs(track_dir)
            
        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(track_dir, "*npz"))
        track_names = [os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(f))[0] for f in files]
        
        current = self.combo_track.currentText()
        
        self.combo_track.blockSignals(True) 
        self.combo_track.clear()
        
        if track_names:
            self.combo_track.addItems(sorted(track_names))
            idx = self.combo_track.findText(current)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_track.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        else:
            self.combo_track.addItem("No tracks found")
            self.combo_track.setEnabled(False)
            
        self.combo_track.blockSignals(False)
        self.on_track_changed()

    def on_track_changed(self):
        """Loads track preview using Track.load instead of from_csv."""
        track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
        if not track_name or track_name == "No tracks found":
            return

        try:
            # Změna: Místo Track.from_csv(filepath) použijeme Track.load(track_name)
            # Track.load si interně sáhne do správné složky (data/tracks)
            self.current_track = Track.load(track_name)
            self.plot_track_map(self.current_track)
            
            # --- FEATURE: Auto-Calculate Laps ---
            dist_m = 22000
            laps = int(round(dist_m / self.current_track.length))
            laps = max(1, laps)
            
            self.input_laps.setValue(laps)
            print(f"Track loaded: {track_name} (L={self.current_track.length:.1f}m) -> Auto-set {laps} laps.")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading track '{track_name}': {e}")
            self.track_plot_widget.clear()
            self.track_plot_widget.setTitle(f"Error: {e}")

    def plot_track_map(self, track: Track):
        # 1. Vyčištění celé figury (smaže i staré colorbary)
        self.mpl_canvas.fig.clf()
        ax = self.mpl_canvas.fig.add_subplot(111)
        
        # 2. Příprava dat
        x = track.x
        y = track.y
        
        # Získání Alpha (sklonu) ve stupních
        if hasattr(track, 'alpha') and track.alpha is not None:
            alpha_deg = np.degrees(track.alpha)
            legend_flag = True
        else:
            alpha_deg = np.zeros_like(x)
            legend_flag = False

        # 3. Segmenty pro LineCollection
        points = np.array([x, y]).T.reshape(-1, 1, 2)
        segments = np.concatenate([points[:-1], points[1:]], axis=1)

        # 4. Nastavení barev (Sklon)
        # Rozsah -10° (modrá) až +10° (červená)
        norm = Normalize(vmin=np.min(alpha_deg), vmax=np.max(alpha_deg))
        lc = LineCollection(segments, cmap='viridis', norm=norm)
        lc.set_array(alpha_deg[:-1])
        lc.set_linewidth(3)
        ax.add_collection(lc)

        # 5. Limity a Aspect Ratio
        ax.set_xlim(x.min() - 20, x.max() + 20)
        ax.set_ylim(y.min() - 20, y.max() + 20)
        ax.set_aspect('equal')

        # 6. Start a Šipka
        ax.plot(x[0], y[0], 'o', color='lime', markersize=8, markeredgecolor='black')

        if hasattr(track, 'psi') and track.psi is not None:
            heading = track.psi[0]
            ax.arrow(x[0], y[0], 
                     np.cos(heading) * 40, np.sin(heading) * 40, 
                     head_width=5, head_length=5, fc='black', ec='black')

        # 7. Legenda (Colorbar)
        # fraction=0.046 a pad=0.04 jsou "magická čísla" v Matplotlibu, 
        # která zajistí, že výška legendy přesně odpovídá výšce grafu.
        if legend_flag:
            cb = self.mpl_canvas.fig.colorbar(lc, ax=ax, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)
            cb.set_label("Incline [deg]", size=10)
            cb.outline.set_visible(False) # Odstraní rámeček kolem legendy pro čistší vzhled

        # 8. Čistý vzhled (bez os)
        ax.axis('off')
        
        self.mpl_canvas.draw()
  
    # ==========================================
    # SIMULATION HANDLERS
    # ==========================================

    def on_run_single_clicked(self):
        print("Preparing SINGLE LAP simulation...")
        try:
            track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
            sim_track = Track.load(track_name)
        except: return

        car = Car.from_ui_widget(self)
        solver = self.create_solver(car, sim_track)
        
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading(text="Running single lap.", indefinite=True)
        
        self.ds_used = self.input_ds.value()
        self.worker = SolverWorker(solver, car, sim_track, self.ds_used, mode=MODE_SINGLE)
        
        self.connect_worker_signals()
        self.worker.start()

    def on_run_endurance_clicked(self):
        print("Preparing ENDURANCE simulation...")
        try:
            track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
            sim_track = Track.load(track_name)
        except: return

        car = Car.from_ui_widget(self)
        laps_count = self.input_laps.value()
        
        # Override car limits for endurance
        batt_kwh = self.input_battery_cap.value()
        car.acp_capacity = batt_kwh # Store for solver usage
        
        solver = self.create_solver(car, sim_track, endurance=True, laps=laps_count)
        
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading(
            text="Starting endurance...", 
            indefinite=True,
            progress_text_fmt="Simulating lap {} / {}..."
        )
        
        self.ds_used = self.input_ds.value()
        self.worker = SolverWorker(solver, car, sim_track, self.ds_used, laps=laps_count, mode=MODE_ENDURANCE)
        
        self.connect_worker_signals()
        self.worker.start()

    def on_endurance_strategy_clicked(self):
       
        try:
            track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
            name = track_name+"_laptime_map"
            tracks_dir="data/tracks/lapmaps"
            filepath = os.path.join(tracks_dir, f"{name}.npz")
            self.laptime_map_plot = LaptimeAppMainWindow(filepath)
            self.laptime_map_plot.optimization_requested.connect(self.start_strategy_optimization)
            self.laptime_map_plot.deployment_requested.connect(self.run_deployment)
            self.laptime_map_plot.show()


        except Exception as e: 
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Could not load laptime map:\n{e}")
            return
        

    def start_strategy_optimization(self, regen_val, delta_val,min_e_target,max_e_target,map_data):
       
        car = Car.from_ui_widget(self)
        laps_count = self.input_laps.value()
        
        laptime_interpolant = map_data['interpolant']
    
        solver = StrategyMaker(car,laptime_interpolant,laps_count,min_e_target+0.01,max_e_target-0.01)

        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Optimizing strategy...", indefinite=True,progress_text_fmt="Solving grid points ({} / {})")
      
        self.ds_used = self.input_ds.value()
        car.regen_eff = regen_val
        car.performance_delta = delta_val
     
        self.worker = SolverWorker(solver, car, None, self.ds_used, mode=MODE_STRATEGY_OPT, laps=laps_count)
        
        self.connect_worker_signals()
        self.worker.progress.connect(self.loading_overlay.update_progress)
        self.worker.progress.connect(self.laptime_map_plot.loading_overlay.update_progress)
        self.worker.start()


    def run_deployment(self,regen_val,delta_val,min_e_target,max_e_target,map_data):

        car = Car.from_ui_widget(self)
        laps_count = self.input_laps.value()
        
        laptime_interpolant = map_data['interpolant']
        solver = StrategyMaker(car, laptime_interpolant, laps_count,min_e_target+0.01,max_e_target-0.01)

        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Calculating lap deployment...", indefinite=True)
        
        self.ds_used = self.input_ds.value()
        car.regen_eff = regen_val
        car.performance_delta = delta_val
     
        # ZMĚNA ZDE: mode=MODE_DEPLOYMENT_OPT
        self.worker = SolverWorker(solver, car, None, self.ds_used, mode=MODE_DEPLOYMENT_OPT, laps=laps_count)
        
        self.connect_worker_signals()
        self.worker.start()

    

        

    def on_acp_test_clicked(self):
        
        try:
             track_name = self.combo_track.currentText()
             sim_track = Track.load(track_name)
        except: return

        car = Car.from_ui_widget(self)
        
        # Ensure we start with correct mass and capacity from UI
        car.acp_capacity = self.input_battery_cap.value()

        laps_count = self.input_laps.value()
        
        solver = MassPointSolver(car, sim_track,laps=laps_count)
        
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading(
            text="Starting sensitivity analysis...", 
            indefinite=True,
            progress_text_fmt="Running lap {} / {}..."
        )
        
        self.ds_used = self.input_ds.value()
        self.worker = SolverWorker(solver, car, sim_track, self.ds_used, mode=MODE_ACP_TEST,laps=laps_count)
        
        self.connect_worker_signals()
        self.worker.start()

    def connect_worker_signals(self):
        """Helper to connect standard worker signals."""
        self.worker.finished.connect(self.on_solver_finished)
        self.worker.error.connect(self.on_solver_error)
        self.worker.progress.connect(self.loading_overlay.update_progress)
        
        

    def on_create_track_clicked(self):
        """
        Wizard pro vytvoření tratě z .mat logu.
        """
        fname, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select telemetry log", "", "MAT Files (*.mat)")
        if not fname: return

        # Loading dat (to je rychlé, může být v main threadu, nebo taky ve workeru, ale nevadí to tolik)
        self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Parsing Log Data...", indefinite=True)
        QApplication.processEvents()

        try:
            data = TrackGenerator.filter_data(fname)
        except Exception as e:
            self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to parse file:\n{e}")
            return
            
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
        if data is None: return

        # Výběr módu
        msg = QMessageBox(self)
        msg.setWindowTitle("Select track mode")
        msg.setText("How do you want to define the track?")
        btn_closed = msg.addButton("Closed track (endurance)", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.AcceptRole)
        btn_open = msg.addButton("Open section (autocross)", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.RejectRole)
        msg.addButton(QMessageBox.StandardButton.Cancel)
        msg.exec()
        
        if msg.clickedButton() == msg.button(QMessageBox.StandardButton.Cancel): return
        is_closed_mode = (msg.clickedButton() == btn_closed)

        lap_info = None
        if is_closed_mode:
            lap_info = TrackGenerator.run_lap_selector(data)
        else:
            lap_info = TrackGenerator.manual_lap_selector(data)

        if not lap_info: return

        # 
        default_name = f"track_{lap_info['time']:.2f}s.npz"
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save Baked Track", 
            os.path.join("data/tracks", default_name), 
            "NumPy Zip (*.npz)"
        )

        if save_path:
        
            self.loading_overlay.show_loading("Smoothing track...", indefinite=True)
            
            self.track_worker = TrackGenerationWorker(data, lap_info, is_closed_mode, save_path)
            self.track_worker.finished.connect(self.on_track_generation_finished)
            self.track_worker.start()

    def on_track_generation_finished(self, success, message):
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
        
        if success:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"Track saved successfully!\n{message}")
            self.load_track_list()
            
            # Automaticky vybrat novou trať v Comboboxu
            track_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(message))[0]
            idx = self.combo_track.findText(track_name)
            if idx >= 0:
                self.combo_track.setCurrentIndex(idx)
        else:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Track generation failed:\n{message}")

    # ==========================================
    # RESULTS HANDLERS
    # ==========================================

    def on_solver_finished(self, res):
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
        
        if hasattr(self, 'laptime_map_plot') and self.laptime_map_plot is not None:
            self.laptime_map_plot.stop_loading()
    
        if res.kind == "single_lap":
            res = res.data
            self.results_window = ResultsWindow(res, self.worker.car, self.worker.track, ds=self.ds_used)
            self.results_window.show()
            return
        
        elif res.kind == "endurance":
            self.results_window = EnduranceResultsWindow(res.data, self.worker.car, self.worker.track, ds=self.ds_used)
            self.results_window.show()
            return
        
        elif res.kind == "drs_test":
            drs_data = res.data['drs']
            nodrs_data = res.data['nodrs']
            self.results_window = DRSResultsWindow(drs_data, nodrs_data)
            self.results_window.show()
            return
        
        elif res.kind == "acp_test":
            self.sens_plotter = LaptimeSensitivityPlot(res.data,laps=self.worker.laps) 
            self.sens_plotter.open_windows()
            return
        
        elif res.kind == "endurance_map":
        
            if hasattr(self, 'laptime_map_plot') and self.laptime_map_plot is not None:
                self.laptime_map_plot.add_result_tab(res)
            return
    
        elif res.kind == "endurance_deployment":
            if hasattr(self, 'laptime_map_plot') and self.laptime_map_plot is not None:
                self.laptime_map_plot.add_result_tab(res)
            return
        
        else:
            print("Optimization failed!")
            return
        

    def on_solver_error(self, error_msg):
        self.loading_overlay.hide_loading()
        if hasattr(self, 'laptime_map_plot') and self.laptime_map_plot is not None:
            self.laptime_map_plot.stop_loading()
        print(f"Solver Error: {error_msg}")

    def on_compare_clicked(self):
        self.compare_window = CompareWindow()
        self.compare_window.show()
